## FILE: `src/wachta/__init__.py`
```
"""Wachta: fatigue and vigilance monitoring from EEG."""

__version__ = "0.1.0"
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/baseline.py`
```
"""Personal calibration: z-scores against the person's own first clean windows."""

import numpy as np


class Baseline:
    """Collects the first ``n_windows`` clean windows and z-scores later ones.

    The centre and spread are robust (median and scaled MAD), so a few blinks
    during calibration do not shift the baseline.
    """

    def __init__(self, keys: tuple[str, ...], n_windows: int) -> None:
        self.keys = keys
        self.n_windows = n_windows
        self._rows: list[list[float]] = []
        self._centre: np.ndarray | None = None
        self._spread: np.ndarray | None = None

    @property
    def ready(self) -> bool:
        return self._centre is not None

    @property
    def progress(self) -> float:
        return 1.0 if self.ready else len(self._rows) / self.n_windows

    def add(self, features: dict[str, float]) -> None:
        """Feed one clean window; ignored once the baseline is ready."""
        if self.ready:
            return
        self._rows.append([features[k] for k in self.keys])
        if len(self._rows) >= self.n_windows:
            rows = np.asarray(self._rows)
            centre = np.median(rows, axis=0)
            spread = np.median(np.abs(rows - centre), axis=0) * 1.4826
            # Guard against a degenerate (flat) calibration signal.
            fallback = rows.std(axis=0)
            spread = np.where(spread > 1e-6, spread, np.where(fallback > 1e-6, fallback, 1.0))
            self._centre, self._spread = centre, spread

    def z(self, features: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
        if not self.ready:
            raise RuntimeError("Baseline is not ready yet")
        values = np.array([features[k] for k in self.keys])
        scores = (values - self._centre) / self._spread
        return dict(zip(self.keys, scores.tolist()))
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/blinks.py`
```
"""Blink detection on the frontal channels, used as evidence that the eyes are open.

With the reference at Fp1 (BrainAccess MINI), a blink shows up as a short,
large deflection on F3/F4. A blink is a run of samples above a personal
threshold lasting 40-500 ms; longer runs are movement, not blinks.
The threshold is a multiple of the median absolute deviation of the frontal
signal during the first seconds of the stream.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.signal import butter, sosfilt, sosfilt_zi

from .config import FRONTAL, Config


class BlinkDetector:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Config) -> None:
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.threshold: float | None = None
        self._calibration: list[np.ndarray] = []
        self._calibration_n = 0
        self._run_start: int | None = None
        self._run_peak = 0.0
        self._last_blink = -10**9
        self.blinks: list[float] = []     # blink times in seconds since stream start
        # Blinks carry most energy below 4 Hz; the low-pass keeps frontal alpha from looking like blinks.
        self._sos = butter(2, cfg.blink_lowpass_hz, "lowpass", fs=cfg.sfreq, output="sos")
        self._zi: np.ndarray | None = None

    @property
    def ready(self) -> bool:
        return self.threshold is not None

    def reset_filter(self) -> None:
        """Forget the filter state and any blink in progress (after a gap or corrupt samples)."""
        self._zi = None
        self._run_start = None

    def push(self, filtered_uv: np.ndarray, first_sample: int) -> list[float]:
        """Feed band-passed EEG (8, n) in µV whose first column is sample ``first_sample``."""
        cfg = self.cfg
        frontal = filtered_uv[list(FRONTAL)].mean(0)
        if frontal.size == 0:
            return []
        if self._zi is None:
            self._zi = sosfilt_zi(self._sos) * frontal[0]
        frontal, self._zi = sosfilt(self._sos, frontal, zi=self._zi)
        if not self.ready:
            start_skip = max(0, int(cfg.settle_s * cfg.sfreq) - first_sample)
            if start_skip < frontal.size:
                self._calibration.append(frontal[start_skip:])
                self._calibration_n += frontal.size - start_skip
            if self._calibration_n >= cfg.blink_cal_s * cfg.sfreq:
                x = np.concatenate(self._calibration)
                mad = np.median(np.abs(x - np.median(x)))
                self.threshold = max(cfg.blink_k * mad, cfg.blink_min_uv)
                self._calibration = []
            return []

        found = []
        above = np.abs(frontal) > self.threshold
        min_len = int(cfg.blink_min_s * cfg.sfreq)
        max_len = int(cfg.blink_max_s * cfg.sfreq)
        refractory = int(cfg.blink_refractory_s * cfg.sfreq)
        for i, flag in enumerate(above):
            sample = first_sample + i
            if flag and self._run_start is None:
                self._run_start = sample
            elif not flag and self._run_start is not None:
                length = sample - self._run_start
                if min_len <= length <= max_len and self._run_start - self._last_blink >= refractory:
                    self._last_blink = self._run_start
                    found.append(self._run_start / cfg.sfreq)
                self._run_start = None
        self.blinks.extend(found)
        if len(self.blinks) > 2000:
            del self.blinks[:1000]
        return found

    def last_blink(self) -> float | None:
        return self.blinks[-1] if self.blinks else None

    def rate_per_min(self, t: float, window_s: float = 60.0) -> float | None:
        if not self.ready:
            return None
        span = min(window_s, max(t - self.cfg.settle_s - self.cfg.blink_cal_s, 1.0))
        return sum(1 for b in self.blinks if t - window_s <= b <= t) * 60.0 / span
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/config.py`
```
"""Shared constants and tunable parameters.

Window sizes and thresholds follow the team's analysis notebooks in
``data/analysis/``. The reserve parameters are assumptions for the demo,
anchored to the EU driving-time rule (4.5 h of driving, then a 45 min break);
they are not fitted to data.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

CHANNELS = ("F3", "F4", "C3", "C4", "P3", "P4", "O1", "O2")
FRONTAL = (0, 1)
PARIETAL = (4, 5)
OCCIPITAL = (6, 7)

BANDS = {"theta": (4.0, 7.0), "alpha": (8.0, 12.0), "beta": (13.0, 30.0)}

# Features that get a calibration baseline and a z-score.
Z_FEATURES = ("alpha_prom", "alpha_occ", "theta_front", "alpha_par", "tab_ratio")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Config:
    # Signal
    sfreq: float = 250.0
    win_s: float = 2.0
    step_s: float = 1.0
    highpass_hz: float = 1.0
    lowpass_hz: float = 40.0
    line_hz: float = 50.0
    settle_s: float = 3.0          # windows ending before this are ignored (filter warm-up)
    reject_uv: float = 300.0       # peak-to-peak above this on any channel marks the window as bad
    flat_uv: float = 0.5           # peak-to-peak below this on all channels: no signal (headset off, frozen stream)

    # Calibration
    cal_windows: int = 60          # first clean windows used as the personal baseline

    # Fast index: vigilance lapses (eyes closing, alpha bursts)
    smooth_windows: int = 5
    # 2.5 was chosen on the four annotated team recordings: all eyes-closed blocks are
    # still detected, with one false alarm in total instead of six at 2.0.
    z_enter: float = 2.5
    z_exit: float = 1.0
    lapse_min_s: float = 3.0
    lapse_exit_s: float = 2.0
    recent_lapse_s: float = 120.0  # a lapse keeps the state at "warn" for this long (real seconds)

    # Eye state from blinks on F3/F4 (A7)
    blink_cal_s: float = 20.0      # seconds of signal used for the blink threshold
    blink_k: float = 5.0           # threshold = k * MAD of the frontal signal
    blink_lowpass_hz: float = 4.0
    blink_min_uv: float = 20.0
    blink_min_s: float = 0.04
    blink_max_s: float = 0.5
    # After the 1 Hz high-pass a blink is biphasic; 0.5 s keeps both lobes as one blink.
    blink_refractory_s: float = 0.5
    eyes_closed_after_s: float = 10.0  # no blink for this long: eyes probably closed
    lapse_blink_lookback_s: float = 2.0  # a blink this soon before the lapse or later: eyes open
    lapse_kind_settle_s: float = 5.0     # count the lapse by the kind it has after this long

    # Acute events (issue #11). Lapses closer than this are one event, e.g. one eye closure
    # interrupted by a short alpha dip. Assumption, not fitted.
    lapse_merge_s: float = 15.0

    # Reaction probes (issue #14): after each acute event and on a schedule, never from a
    # sub-threshold alpha rise (notebook 06: alpha with open eyes does not predict lapses).
    probe_interval_s: float = 1200.0   # scheduled probe every 20 min of real time (assumption)
    probe_event_delay_s: float = 12.0  # probe after an event only once alpha stayed low this long;
                                       # dips inside one eye closure lasted 6-11 s in our recordings
    probe_cooldown_s: float = 30.0     # at least this long between two probes
    probe_lapse_ms: float = 355.0      # PVT-B lapse threshold (Basner et al. 2011) until a personal norm exists
    probe_norm_factor: float = 1.5     # personal norm: lapse above 1.5 x own median RT (as in Cui et al.)
    probe_norm_min: int = 3            # passed responses needed before the personal norm is used
    probe_timeout_ms: float = 3000.0
    probe_min_rt_ms: float = 100.0     # faster than this is a false start (anticipation), not a measured reaction (PVT convention)
    probe_stop_s: float = 120.0        # a failed probe keeps "stop" until a passed probe or this long

    # Slow index: fatigue trend
    slow_window_s: float = 60.0

    # Signal quality
    quality_window_s: float = 30.0
    quality_min: float = 0.5

    # Reserve (assumptions, see module docstring)
    time_scale: float = 1.0        # shift seconds per real second (demo time compression)
    drain_per_hour: float = 12.0   # baseline: 100 -> 46 after 4.5 h of work
    # The slow index (theta + alpha) / beta is not validated on our hardware (notebooks 03 and 04: it fails with the
    # Fp1 reference), so it does not change the reserve by default (issue #19). Kept as a knob for experiments.
    slow_gain: float = 0.0         # extra drain per sigma of the slow index
    slow_cap: float = 3.0
    lapse_penalty: float = 8.0     # reserve points lost per lapse
    recharge_per_hour: float = 60.0  # a 45 min break restores 45 points
    warn_below: float = 50.0
    stop_below: float = 25.0

    # Context (A9): simplified two-process model, assumptions. Off unless start_clock_h is set.
    start_clock_h: float | None = None     # time of day at calibration, in hours
    awake_at_start_h: float | None = None  # hours awake at calibration
    circadian_night_gain: float = 0.6      # drain +60% around the circadian low ...
    circadian_night_h: float = 4.5         # ... centred at 04:30
    circadian_night_width_h: float = 2.5
    circadian_dip_gain: float = 0.2        # post-lunch dip
    circadian_dip_h: float = 15.0
    circadian_dip_width_h: float = 1.5
    awake_gain_per_h: float = 0.06         # +6% drain per hour awake beyond awake_free_h
    awake_free_h: float = 8.0

    @property
    def win_samples(self) -> int:
        return int(round(self.win_s * self.sfreq))

    @property
    def step_samples(self) -> int:
        return int(round(self.step_s * self.sfreq))
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/features.py`
```
"""Spectral features of one EEG window.

The feature definitions come from the notebooks in ``data/analysis/``:
band power per region (notebook 03) and alpha prominence, i.e. alpha power
relative to its spectral neighbours (notebook 01).
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.signal import welch

from .config import BANDS, FRONTAL, OCCIPITAL, PARIETAL

_EPS = 1e-12


def _band_power(psd: np.ndarray, freqs: np.ndarray, lo: float, hi: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Integrated power in [lo, hi] Hz for each channel, in µV²."""
    mask = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs <= hi)
    return psd[..., mask].sum(-1) * (freqs[1] - freqs[0])


def _band_mean(psd: np.ndarray, freqs: np.ndarray, lo: float, hi: float) -> float:
    mask = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs <= hi)
    return float(psd[..., mask].mean())


def window_features(window_uv: np.ndarray, sfreq: float) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Compute features for one window.

    Parameters
    ----------
    window_uv : array, shape (8, n_samples)
        Band-pass filtered EEG in microvolts, channels ordered as ``config.CHANNELS``.
    sfreq : float
        Sampling rate in Hz.
    """
    n = window_uv.shape[-1]
    freqs, psd = welch(window_uv, sfreq, nperseg=n, axis=-1)
    power = {name: _band_power(psd, freqs, lo, hi) for name, (lo, hi) in BANDS.items()}

    occ = psd[list(OCCIPITAL)].mean(0)
    neighbours = np.sqrt(_band_mean(occ, freqs, 5, 7) * _band_mean(occ, freqs, 14, 16))
    alpha_prom = np.log10((_band_mean(occ, freqs, 8, 12) + _EPS) / (neighbours + _EPS))

    slow_waves = power["theta"].mean() + power["alpha"].mean()
    return {
        "alpha_prom": float(alpha_prom),
        "alpha_occ": float(np.log10(power["alpha"][list(OCCIPITAL)].mean() + _EPS)),
        "theta_front": float(np.log10(power["theta"][list(FRONTAL)].mean() + _EPS)),
        "alpha_par": float(np.log10(power["alpha"][list(PARIETAL)].mean() + _EPS)),
        "tab_ratio": float(np.log10((slow_waves + _EPS) / (power["beta"].mean() + _EPS))),
        "ptp_uv": float(np.ptp(window_uv, axis=-1).max()),
    }
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/index.py`
```
"""Vigilance index: lapse detection, eye state, reaction probes, reserve and state.

Signals that feed the state:

* fast: z-score of alpha prominence over the occipital channels. A sustained
  rise is an acute event: eyes closing or an alpha wave right now. It detects,
  it does not predict (notebook 06: alpha with open eyes does not forecast lapses).
  Lapses closer than ``lapse_merge_s`` are one event (issue #11).
* eye state: blinks on the frontal channels (``blinks.py``). A lapse without
  blinks is "eyes closed" (what a driver camera also sees); a lapse while the
  person keeps blinking is labelled "eyes open".
* reaction probes (issue #14): after each acute event and on a schedule. A missing
  response or one slower than the personal norm (1.5 x own median, PVT-B 355 ms
  until enough responses) is a failed probe.
* slow: one-minute mean of the z-scored (theta + alpha) / beta ratio. It only
  changes how fast the reserve drains.
* context: hours awake and time of day (simplified two-process model). Also
  only changes the drain rate.

The reserve and context parameters are assumptions, not fitted values
(see ``config.Config``).
"""

import math
from collections import deque

from .config import Config

STATES = ("calibrating", "no_signal", "break", "ok", "warn", "stop")


def _circular_bump(hour: float, centre: float, width: float) -> float:
    distance = abs((hour - centre + 12.0) % 24.0 - 12.0)
    return math.exp(-0.5 * (distance / width) ** 2)


class VigilanceIndex:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Config) -> None:
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.reserve = 100.0
        self.on_break = False
        self.work_s = 0.0          # shift seconds worked since calibration
        self.shift_s = 0.0         # shift seconds since calibration, breaks included
        self.lapse_count = 0
        self.lapse_kinds = {"eyes_closed": 0, "eyes_open": 0, "unknown": 0}
        self.probes_done = 0
        self.probe_lapses = 0
        self.last_rt_ms: float | None = None
        self._passed_rts: list[float] = []
        self._probe_requested = False
        self._probe_reason: str | None = None
        self._last_probe_t = -math.inf
        self._next_scheduled_t: float | None = None
        self._failed_probe_t = -math.inf
        self.reset_detection()

    def reset_detection(self) -> None:
        """Forget signal history (used on recalibration); reserve and shift time stay."""
        self._fast_hist: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=self.cfg.smooth_windows)
        self._slow_hist: deque[tuple[float, float]] = deque()
        self._quality_hist: deque[tuple[float, bool]] = deque()
        self._lapse_starts: deque[float] = deque()
        self._above = 0
        self._below = 0
        self.lapse = False
        self.lapse_kind: str | None = None
        self._lapse_t0 = 0.0
        self._counted_kind: str | None = None
        self._last_lapse_end = -math.inf
        self._event_probe_pending = False

    def start_break(self) -> None:
        """Rest is not work: eyes closed during a break is neither an event nor a penalty."""
        self.on_break = True
        self.lapse = False
        self.lapse_kind = None
        self._above = self._below = 0
        self._event_probe_pending = False
        self._probe_requested = False

    def end_break(self) -> None:
        self.on_break = False

    def request_probe(self) -> None:
        """Ask for a reaction probe at the next window (manual check)."""
        self._probe_requested = True

    def probe_threshold_ms(self) -> float:
        """Reaction time above which a probe fails: personal norm once known, else the PVT-B lapse."""
        cfg = self.cfg
        if len(self._passed_rts) < cfg.probe_norm_min:
            return cfg.probe_lapse_ms
        rts = sorted(self._passed_rts)
        mid = len(rts) // 2
        median = rts[mid] if len(rts) % 2 else 0.5 * (rts[mid - 1] + rts[mid])
        return cfg.probe_norm_factor * median

    def record_probe(self, t: float, rt_ms: float | None) -> bool:
        """Store a probe result; returns True when the probe failed.

        A reaction that is not a number, negative or faster than ``probe_min_rt_ms`` is a false start or bad
        input: it is not counted at all, neither as a pass nor as a lapse.
        """
        cfg = self.cfg
        if rt_ms is not None and not (math.isfinite(rt_ms) and rt_ms >= cfg.probe_min_rt_ms):
            return False
        threshold = self.probe_threshold_ms()
        self.probes_done += 1
        self.last_rt_ms = rt_ms
        failed = rt_ms is None or rt_ms > threshold
        if failed:
            self.probe_lapses += 1
            self.reserve = max(0.0, self.reserve - cfg.lapse_penalty)
            self._lapse_starts.append(t)
            self._failed_probe_t = t
        else:
            self._passed_rts.append(float(rt_ms))
            self._failed_probe_t = -math.inf        # a passed probe clears the stop
        return failed

    # -- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------

    def _fast(self) -> float:
        valid = sorted(v for v in self._fast_hist if not math.isnan(v))
        if len(valid) < (self.cfg.smooth_windows + 1) // 2:
            return math.nan
        mid = len(valid) // 2
        return valid[mid] if len(valid) % 2 else 0.5 * (valid[mid - 1] + valid[mid])

    def _slow(self, t: float) -> float:
        while self._slow_hist and self._slow_hist[0][0] < t - self.cfg.slow_window_s:
            self._slow_hist.popleft()
        if len(self._slow_hist) < 10:
            return 0.0
        return sum(v for _, v in self._slow_hist) / len(self._slow_hist)

    def _quality(self, t: float) -> float:
        while self._quality_hist and self._quality_hist[0][0] < t - self.cfg.quality_window_s:
            self._quality_hist.popleft()
        if not self._quality_hist:
            return 0.0
        return sum(1 for _, clean in self._quality_hist if clean) / len(self._quality_hist)

    def _update_lapse(self, t: float, fast: float) -> bool:
        """Hysteresis on the fast index. Returns True when a new acute event starts.

        A lapse that starts within ``lapse_merge_s`` of the previous one continues that
        event: no new count, no new penalty, no new probe.
        """
        cfg = self.cfg
        if math.isnan(fast):
            self._above = 0
            return False
        self._above = self._above + 1 if fast > cfg.z_enter else 0
        self._below = self._below + 1 if fast < cfg.z_exit else 0
        if not self.lapse and self._above * cfg.step_s >= cfg.lapse_min_s:
            self.lapse = True
            if t - self._last_lapse_end < cfg.lapse_merge_s:
                return False
            self.lapse_count += 1
            self._lapse_t0 = t
            self._lapse_starts.append(t)
            self._counted_kind = None
            self._event_probe_pending = True
            return True
        if self.lapse and self._below * cfg.step_s >= cfg.lapse_exit_s:
            self.lapse = False
            self._last_lapse_end = t
        return False

    def _eye_state(self, t: float, last_blink: float | None, blinks_ready: bool) -> str | None:
        if not blinks_ready:
            return None
        if last_blink is None or t - last_blink >= self.cfg.eyes_closed_after_s:
            return "closed"
        return "open"

    def _classify_lapse(self, t: float, last_blink: float | None, blinks_ready: bool) -> str:
        """Eyes closed if there was no blink since shortly before the lapse began."""
        if not blinks_ready:
            return "unknown"
        since = self._lapse_t0 - self.cfg.lapse_blink_lookback_s
        return "eyes_open" if last_blink is not None and last_blink >= since else "eyes_closed"

    def _context(self) -> tuple[float, float | None, float | None]:
        """Drain multiplier from hours awake and time of day, plus the two values."""
        cfg = self.cfg
        if cfg.start_clock_h is None:
            return 1.0, None, None
        clock = (cfg.start_clock_h + self.shift_s / 3600.0) % 24.0
        factor = (1.0 + cfg.circadian_night_gain * _circular_bump(clock, cfg.circadian_night_h, cfg.circadian_night_width_h)
                  + cfg.circadian_dip_gain * _circular_bump(clock, cfg.circadian_dip_h, cfg.circadian_dip_width_h))
        awake = None
        if cfg.awake_at_start_h is not None:
            awake = cfg.awake_at_start_h + self.shift_s / 3600.0
            factor *= 1.0 + cfg.awake_gain_per_h * max(0.0, awake - cfg.awake_free_h)
        return factor, clock, awake

    # -- main entry point ------------------------------------------------

    def update(
        self,
        t: float,
        z: dict[str, float] | None,
        bad: bool,
        calibrated: bool,
        last_blink: float | None = None,
        blinks_ready: bool = False,
    ) -> dict:
        """Advance by one window.

        ``t`` is the window end in real seconds since the stream started,
        ``z`` holds the z-scored features of a clean, calibrated window (else None),
        ``last_blink`` is the time of the most recent blink in the same clock.
        """
        cfg = self.cfg
        self._quality_hist.append((t, not bad))
        quality = self._quality(t)

        if z is not None:
            self._fast_hist.append(z["alpha_prom"])
            self._slow_hist.append((t, z["tab_ratio"]))
        else:
            self._fast_hist.append(math.nan)
        fast = self._fast()
        slow = self._slow(t)

        if self.on_break:
            self._above = self._below = 0          # no events while resting
        new_lapse = self._update_lapse(t, fast) if calibrated and not self.on_break else False
        if self.lapse:
            self.lapse_kind = self._classify_lapse(t, last_blink, blinks_ready)
            # Count each event once, by the kind it settles on after the confirmation period.
            if self._counted_kind is None and t - self._lapse_t0 >= cfg.lapse_kind_settle_s:
                self._counted_kind = self.lapse_kind
                self.lapse_kinds[self.lapse_kind] += 1
        else:
            if self._counted_kind is None and self.lapse_kind is not None:
                self._counted_kind = self.lapse_kind
                self.lapse_kinds[self.lapse_kind] += 1
            self.lapse_kind = None

        # Reaction probe: right after an acute event ends, on the schedule, or on request.
        probe, probe_reason = False, None
        if calibrated and self._next_scheduled_t is None:
            self._next_scheduled_t = t + cfg.probe_interval_s
        if calibrated and not self.on_break and quality >= cfg.quality_min and not self.lapse:
            cooled = t - self._last_probe_t >= cfg.probe_cooldown_s
            if self._probe_requested:
                probe_reason = "manual"
            elif self._event_probe_pending and cooled and t - self._last_lapse_end >= cfg.probe_event_delay_s:
                probe_reason = "event"
            elif self._next_scheduled_t is not None and t >= self._next_scheduled_t and cooled:
                probe_reason = "schedule"
            if probe_reason is not None:
                probe = True
                self._probe_requested = False
                self._event_probe_pending = False
                self._last_probe_t = t
                self._next_scheduled_t = t + cfg.probe_interval_s

        while self._lapse_starts and self._lapse_starts[0] < t - cfg.recent_lapse_s:
            self._lapse_starts.popleft()
        recent = len(self._lapse_starts)

        context, clock, awake = self._context()
        rate = cfg.drain_per_hour * context * (1.0 + cfg.slow_gain * min(max(slow, 0.0), cfg.slow_cap))
        if calibrated:
            shift_dt = cfg.step_s * cfg.time_scale
            self.shift_s += shift_dt
            if self.on_break:
                self.reserve += cfg.recharge_per_hour * shift_dt / 3600.0
            else:
                self.reserve -= rate * shift_dt / 3600.0
                self.work_s += shift_dt
            if new_lapse:
                self.reserve -= cfg.lapse_penalty
            self.reserve = min(100.0, max(0.0, self.reserve))

        if not calibrated:
            state = "calibrating"
        elif quality < cfg.quality_min:
            state = "no_signal"
        elif self.on_break:
            state = "break"
        elif self.lapse or self.reserve < cfg.stop_below or t - self._failed_probe_t < cfg.probe_stop_s:
            # "Stop" means now: a lapse in progress, an exhausted reserve or a failed reaction probe.
            state = "stop"
        elif self.reserve < cfg.warn_below or recent >= 1:
            # Recent events lower the reserve and raise attention, but do not hold "stop" (issue #11).
            state = "warn"
        else:
            state = "ok"

        def minutes_to(level: float) -> float | None:
            if self.on_break or not calibrated or self.reserve <= level:
                return None
            return (self.reserve - level) / rate * 60.0

        return {
            "state": state,
            "reserve": round(self.reserve, 2),
            "fast": None if math.isnan(fast) else round(fast, 3),
            "slow": round(slow, 3),
            "lapse": self.lapse,
            "lapse_kind": self.lapse_kind,
            "new_lapse": new_lapse,
            "lapses_recent": recent,
            "lapse_count": self.lapse_count,
            "lapses_eyes_closed": self.lapse_kinds["eyes_closed"],
            "lapses_eyes_open": self.lapse_kinds["eyes_open"],
            # During a lapse the eye state follows the lapse kind, so the banner and the tile agree.
            "eye_state": {"eyes_closed": "closed", "eyes_open": "open"}.get(
                self.lapse_kind, self._eye_state(t, last_blink, blinks_ready)
            ),
            "probe": probe,
            "probe_reason": probe_reason,
            "probe_threshold_ms": round(self.probe_threshold_ms(), 1),
            "probe_failed_recently": t - self._failed_probe_t < cfg.probe_stop_s,
            "probes_done": self.probes_done,
            "probe_lapses": self.probe_lapses,
            "last_rt_ms": self.last_rt_ms,
            "quality": round(quality, 3),
            "drain_per_hour": round(rate, 2),
            "context_factor": round(context, 3),
            "clock_h": None if clock is None else round(clock, 3),
            "awake_h": None if awake is None else round(awake, 2),
            "minutes_to_warn": minutes_to(cfg.warn_below),
            "minutes_to_stop": minutes_to(cfg.stop_below),
            "work_min": round(self.work_s / 60.0, 2),
            "on_break": self.on_break,
        }
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/night.py`
```
"""Analysis of checkpoint recordings with a monotony block (night protocol, task C4).

For every recording whose annotations contain a ``monotony`` block, the recording
goes through the same pipeline as the demo (calibration on the ``eyes_open``
block) and we summarise how the vigilance index behaves during monotonous
sitting with open eyes: early versus late minutes, reaction probes the system
would ask for, lapses and their kind, blink rate. Results are joined with KSS and
PVT-B from ``checkpoints.csv`` and ``pvt.csv``. A Garmin FIT file recorded in
parallel (chest strap paired with the watch) adds heart rate and, if the file
contains beat-to-beat intervals, RMSSD per block.

Run with:
    python experiments/analyze-night-checkpoint.py
    python experiments/analyze-night-checkpoint.py --fit wojtek-n1=~/Downloads/activity.fit --figure docs/img/noc-vs-dzien.png
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Sequence

import numpy as np

from .config import Config
from .pipeline import run_offline
from .recordings import Block

EARLY_LATE_S = 120.0          # compare the first and the last two minutes of monotony
RR_MIN_MS, RR_MAX_MS = 300.0, 2000.0


def _mean(values: Iterable[float | None]) -> float | None:
    kept = [v for v in values if v is not None and not (isinstance(v, float) and math.isnan(v))]
    return float(np.mean(kept)) if kept else None


def task_parts(task: str) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
    """``wojtek-n1`` -> (``wojtek``, ``N1``); None when the task name has no checkpoint."""
    person, sep, checkpoint = task.rpartition("-")
    if not sep or not person or not checkpoint:
        return None
    return person.lower(), checkpoint.upper()


def analyze_signals(eeg_volts: np.ndarray, blocks: Sequence[Block], cfg: Config | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Summarise one recording; returns None when it has no ``monotony`` block."""
    cfg = cfg or Config()
    monotony = next((b for b in blocks if b.label == "monotony"), None)
    if monotony is None:
        return None
    results = run_offline(eeg_volts, cfg)
    calibrated = [r for r in results if r["calibrated"]]

    def window_in(r: dict, start: float, end: float) -> bool:
        return start + cfg.win_s <= r["t"] <= end

    closed = next((b for b in blocks if b.label == "eyes_closed"), None)
    eyes_closed_detected = None
    if closed is not None:
        eyes_closed_detected = any(r["new_lapse"] and closed.onset <= r["t"] <= closed.end for r in calibrated)

    inside = [r for r in results if window_in(r, monotony.onset, monotony.end)]
    early = [r for r in inside if r["t"] <= monotony.onset + EARLY_LATE_S]
    late = [r for r in inside if r["t"] >= monotony.end - EARLY_LATE_S]

    # One kind per lapse: the kind most windows of that lapse had.
    lapses, current = [], []
    for r in inside:
        if r["lapse"]:
            current.append(r["lapse_kind"] or "unknown")
        elif current:
            lapses.append(max(set(current), key=current.count))
            current = []
    if current:
        lapses.append(max(set(current), key=current.count))

    def blink_rate_at(t: float) -> float | None:
        before = [r for r in results if r["t"] <= t]
        return before[-1]["blinks_per_min"] if before else None

    # Minute-by-minute course of the monotony block (the main view for a single person).
    minutes = []
    n_minutes = int(round(monotony.duration / 60.0))
    for k in range(n_minutes):
        start, end = monotony.onset + 60.0 * k, monotony.onset + 60.0 * (k + 1)
        in_minute = [r for r in results if start < r["t"] <= end]
        fast = _mean(r["fast"] for r in in_minute)
        minutes.append({
            "minute": k + 1,
            "fast": None if fast is None else round(fast, 2),
            "blinks": blink_rate_at(end),
            "rejected_pct": round(100.0 * np.mean([r["bad"] for r in in_minute]), 1) if in_minute else None,
            "lapse": any(r["lapse"] for r in in_minute),
        })

    fast_early = _mean(r["fast"] for r in early)
    fast_late = _mean(r["fast"] for r in late)
    return {
        "calibrated": bool(calibrated),
        "eyes_closed_detected": eyes_closed_detected,
        "monotony_min": round(monotony.duration / 60.0, 1),
        "rejected_pct": round(100.0 * np.mean([r["bad"] for r in inside]), 1) if inside else None,
        "fast_early": None if fast_early is None else round(fast_early, 2),
        "fast_late": None if fast_late is None else round(fast_late, 2),
        "fast_change": None if fast_early is None or fast_late is None else round(fast_late - fast_early, 2),
        "probes": sum(1 for r in inside if r["probe"]),
        "lapses": len(lapses),
        "lapses_eyes_open": lapses.count("eyes_open"),
        "lapses_eyes_closed": lapses.count("eyes_closed"),
        "blinks_early": blink_rate_at(monotony.onset + EARLY_LATE_S),
        "blinks_late": blink_rate_at(monotony.end),
        "minutes": minutes,
    }


# -- Garmin FIT (chest strap paired with the watch) ---------------------------------


def heart_from_frames(frames: Iterable[Any]) -> tuple[list[tuple[datetime, float]], list[tuple[datetime, float]]]:
    """Extract (time, bpm) and (time, RR ms) from decoded FIT data messages.

    ``record`` messages give heart rate with timestamps. ``hrv`` messages give
    beat-to-beat intervals without timestamps; they are placed by accumulating the
    intervals from the first record timestamp, which is accurate to a few seconds.
    """
    heart_rate: list[tuple[datetime, float]] = []
    intervals: list[float] = []
    for frame in frames:
        name = getattr(frame, "name", None)
        if name == "record":
            stamp = frame.get_value("timestamp", fallback=None)
            bpm = frame.get_value("heart_rate", fallback=None)
            if stamp is not None and bpm is not None:
                heart_rate.append((stamp, float(bpm)))
        elif name == "hrv":
            values = frame.get_value("time", fallback=None)
            if values is None:
                continue
            for value in values if isinstance(values, (list, tuple)) else [values]:
                if value is not None and value < 60:          # seconds; 65.535 marks "invalid"
                    intervals.append(float(value) * 1000.0)
    rr: list[tuple[datetime, float]] = []
    if intervals and heart_rate:
        clock = heart_rate[0][0]
        for ms in intervals:
            clock = clock + timedelta(milliseconds=ms)
            rr.append((clock, ms))
    return heart_rate, rr


def load_fit(path: Path | str) -> tuple[list[tuple[datetime, float]], list[tuple[datetime, float]]]:
    try:
        import fitdecode
    except ImportError as error:
        raise RuntimeError('Brak biblioteki fitdecode. Zainstaluj: pip install -e ".[analysis]"') from error
    with fitdecode.FitReader(str(path)) as reader:
        frames = [f for f in reader if isinstance(f, fitdecode.FitDataMessage)]
    return heart_from_frames(frames)


def rmssd(rr_ms: Sequence[float]) -> float | None:
    """RMSSD of plausible intervals; successive jumps over 20% are treated as artefacts."""
    clean = [v for v in rr_ms if RR_MIN_MS <= v <= RR_MAX_MS]
    diffs = [b - a for a, b in zip(clean, clean[1:]) if abs(b - a) <= 0.2 * a]
    if len(diffs) < 10:
        return None
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(diffs))))


def heart_per_block(
    started_at: datetime,
    blocks: Sequence[Block],
    heart_rate: Sequence[tuple[datetime, float]],
    rr: Sequence[tuple[datetime, float]],
) -> dict[str, dict[str, float | None]]:
    """Mean heart rate and RMSSD for every protocol block, by wall-clock time."""
    out = {}
    for block in blocks:
        start = started_at + timedelta(seconds=block.onset)
        end = started_at + timedelta(seconds=block.end)
        hr = [v for t, v in heart_rate if start <= t < end]
        beats = [v for t, v in rr if start <= t < end]
        out[block.label] = {
            "hr": None if not hr else round(float(np.mean(hr)), 1),
            "rmssd": None if (value := rmssd(beats)) is None else round(value, 1),
        }
    return out


def heart_per_minute(
    started_at: datetime, block: Block, heart_rate: Sequence[tuple[datetime, float]]
) -> list[float | None]:
    """Mean heart rate in each minute of one block."""
    out = []
    for k in range(int(round(block.duration / 60.0))):
        start = started_at + timedelta(seconds=block.onset + 60.0 * k)
        values = [v for t, v in heart_rate if start <= t < start + timedelta(seconds=60)]
        out.append(None if not values else round(float(np.mean(values)), 1))
    return out


def parse_started_at(text: str) -> datetime | None:
    try:
        stamp = datetime.fromisoformat(text)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return stamp if stamp.tzinfo else stamp.astimezone()


# -- report ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _pl(value: Any, digits: int = 1, signed: bool = False) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return "—"
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "tak" if value else "nie"
    if isinstance(value, int):
        return f"{value:+d}" if signed else str(value)
    text = f"{value:+.{digits}f}" if signed else f"{value:.{digits}f}"
    return text.replace(".", ",")


def format_table(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    head = ("| Osoba | Punkt | KSS | PVT: lapsy | PVT: mediana RT | Alfa (z) min. 1–2 | Alfa (z) ostatnie 2 min | Zmiana | "
            "Próby reakcji | Spadki (oczy otwarte / zamknięte) | Mrugnięcia/min początek → koniec | "
            "Tętno spoczynek → monotonia | RMSSD spoczynek → monotonia | Oczy zamknięte wykryte |")
    lines = [head, "|" + "---|" * (head.count("|") - 1)]
    for r in rows:
        heart = r.get("heart") or {}
        rest, mono = heart.get("eyes_open", {}), heart.get("monotony", {})
        lines.append(
            f"| {r['person']} | {r['checkpoint']} | {_pl(r.get('kss'))} | {_pl(r.get('pvt_lapses'))} | "
            f"{_pl(r.get('median_rt_ms'), 0)} ms | {_pl(r.get('fast_early'), 2)} | {_pl(r.get('fast_late'), 2)} | "
            f"{_pl(r.get('fast_change'), 2, signed=True)} | {_pl(r.get('probes'))} | "
            f"{r.get('lapses_eyes_open', '—')} / {r.get('lapses_eyes_closed', '—')} | "
            f"{_pl(r.get('blinks_early'), 0)} → {_pl(r.get('blinks_late'), 0)} | "
            f"{_pl(rest.get('hr'), 0)} → {_pl(mono.get('hr'), 0)} | {_pl(rest.get('rmssd'), 0)} → {_pl(mono.get('rmssd'), 0)} | "
            f"{_pl(r.get('eyes_closed_detected'))} |"
        )
    return "\n".join(lines)


def save_figure(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], path: Path | str) -> Path:
    """Three small panels per checkpoint, one line per person: KSS, PVT lapses, late-monotony alpha."""
    import matplotlib

    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    surface, ink, ink2, muted, grid = "#fcfcfb", "#0b0b0b", "#52514e", "#898781", "#e1e0d9"
    palette = ["#2a78d6", "#eb6834", "#1baf7a", "#6250d6"]
    people = sorted({r["person"] for r in rows})
    order = sorted({r["checkpoint"] for r in rows}, key=lambda c: (c[0] != "T", c))
    panels = [("kss", "Senność (KSS, 1–9)"), ("pvt_lapses", "Lapsy w teście reakcji"), ("fast_late", "Alfa w monotonii (z), ostatnie 2 min")]

    plt.rcParams.update({"font.family": "DejaVu Sans", "font.size": 9})
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(11, 3.6), facecolor=surface)
    for ax, (key, title) in zip(axes, panels):
        ax.set_facecolor(surface)
        for i, person in enumerate(people):
            series = [(order.index(r["checkpoint"]), r.get(key)) for r in rows if r["person"] == person and r.get(key) is not None]
            series.sort()
            if not series:
                continue
            xs, ys = zip(*series)
            color = palette[i % len(palette)]
            ax.plot(xs, ys, color=color, lw=2, marker="o", ms=6, mec=surface, mew=2)
            ax.annotate(person, (xs[-1], ys[-1]), xytext=(6, 0), textcoords="offset points", va="center", fontsize=8, color=ink2)
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(order)), order)
        if key != "fast_late":
            from matplotlib.ticker import MaxNLocator
            ax.yaxis.set_major_locator(MaxNLocator(integer=True))
        ax.set_title(title, loc="left", fontsize=9.5, color=ink, fontweight="bold")
        ax.grid(axis="y", color=grid, lw=0.8)
        ax.set_axisbelow(True)
        ax.tick_params(colors=muted, length=0)
        for side in ("top", "right", "left"):
            ax.spines[side].set_visible(False)
        ax.spines["bottom"].set_color("#c3c2b7")
        ax.set_xlim(-0.3, len(order) - 0.4)
    fig.suptitle("Dzień (T) a noc (N): ocena senności, test reakcji i EEG", x=0.01, ha="left", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", color=ink)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.92))
    out = Path(path)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(out, dpi=130, facecolor=surface)
    plt.close(fig)
    return out


def save_minutes_figure(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], path: Path | str) -> Path:
    """Minute-by-minute monotony course, one line per session: alpha index, blinks and heart rate."""
    import matplotlib

    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    surface, ink, ink2, muted, grid = "#fcfcfb", "#0b0b0b", "#52514e", "#898781", "#e1e0d9"
    palette = ["#2a78d6", "#eb6834", "#1baf7a", "#6250d6"]
    sessions = sorted(rows, key=lambda r: (r["person"], r["checkpoint"][0] != "T", r["checkpoint"]))
    several_people = len({r["person"] for r in rows}) > 1
    has_heart = any(r.get("hr_minutes") for r in rows)
    panels = [("fast", "Wskaźnik alfy (z-score względem kalibracji)"), ("blinks", "Mrugnięcia na minutę")]
    if has_heart:
        panels.append(("hr", "Tętno z paska [bpm]"))

    plt.rcParams.update({"font.family": "DejaVu Sans", "font.size": 9})
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, len(panels), figsize=(3.9 * len(panels), 3.7), facecolor=surface)
    for ax, (key, title) in zip(axes, panels):
        ax.set_facecolor(surface)
        for i, row in enumerate(sessions[: len(palette)]):
            label = f"{row['person']} {row['checkpoint']}" if several_people else row["checkpoint"]
            if key == "hr":
                values = list(enumerate(row.get("hr_minutes") or [], start=1))
            else:
                values = [(m["minute"], m[key]) for m in row.get("minutes", [])]
            points = [(x, y) for x, y in values if y is not None]
            if not points:
                continue
            xs, ys = zip(*points)
            color = palette[i]
            ax.plot(xs, ys, color=color, lw=2, marker="o", ms=5, mec=surface, mew=1.5)
            ax.annotate(label, (xs[-1], ys[-1]), xytext=(6, 0), textcoords="offset points", va="center", fontsize=8, color=ink2)
            if key == "fast":
                for m in row.get("minutes", []):
                    if m["lapse"] and m["fast"] is not None:
                        ax.plot(m["minute"], m["fast"], marker="o", ms=9, mfc="none", mec="#d03b3b", mew=1.8)
        if key == "fast":
            ax.axhline(0, color="#c3c2b7", lw=1)
            threshold = Config().z_enter
            ax.axhline(threshold, color=ink2, lw=1)
            ax.annotate(f"próg alarmu {threshold:g}".replace(".", ","), (1, threshold), xytext=(0, 4),
                        textcoords="offset points", fontsize=8, color=ink2)
            low, high = ax.get_ylim()
            ax.set_ylim(min(low, -0.5), max(high, threshold + 0.8))
        if key == "blinks":
            ax.set_ylim(bottom=0, top=max(10.0, ax.get_ylim()[1] * 1.1))
        ax.set_title(title, loc="left", fontsize=9.5, color=ink, fontweight="bold")
        ax.set_xlabel("minuta monotonii", color=ink2, fontsize=8.5)
        ax.grid(axis="y", color=grid, lw=0.8)
        ax.set_axisbelow(True)
        ax.tick_params(colors=muted, length=0)
        for side in ("top", "right", "left"):
            ax.spines[side].set_visible(False)
        ax.spines["bottom"].set_color("#c3c2b7")
    fig.suptitle("Monotonia minuta po minucie", x=0.01, ha="left", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", color=ink)
    fig.text(0.01, 0.885, "Czerwone kółko: minuta z wykrytym spadkiem czujności.", fontsize=8.5, color=ink2)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.86))
    out = Path(path)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(out, dpi=130, facecolor=surface)
    plt.close(fig)
    return out
```

## FILE: `src/wachta/pipeline.py`
```
"""Streaming pipeline: raw EEG chunks in, one result per one-second step out.

The same code runs on live data and on recordings, so offline accuracy numbers
describe what the demo actually does.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.signal import butter, iirnotch, sosfilt, sosfilt_zi, tf2sos

from .baseline import Baseline
from .blinks import BlinkDetector
from .config import CHANNELS, Z_FEATURES, Config
from .features import window_features
from .index import VigilanceIndex


class StreamFilter:
    """Causal 1-40 Hz band-pass with a mains notch, applied chunk by chunk.

    Dry electrodes drift by tens of millivolts, so the filter state starts at
    the steady state for the first sample to avoid a start-up transient.
    """

    def __init__(self, cfg: Config) -> None:
        highpass = butter(4, cfg.highpass_hz, "highpass", fs=cfg.sfreq, output="sos")
        lowpass = butter(8, cfg.lowpass_hz, "lowpass", fs=cfg.sfreq, output="sos")
        notch = tf2sos(*iirnotch(cfg.line_hz, 30.0, fs=cfg.sfreq))
        self._sos = np.vstack([highpass, lowpass, notch])
        self._zi: np.ndarray | None = None

    def __call__(self, chunk: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        if chunk.shape[1] == 0:
            return chunk
        if self._zi is None:
            self._zi = sosfilt_zi(self._sos)[:, None, :] * chunk[None, :, :1]
        out, self._zi = sosfilt(self._sos, chunk, axis=-1, zi=self._zi)
        return out


class Pipeline:
    """Turns a stream of raw EEG into features, z-scores, state and reserve."""

    def __init__(self, cfg: Config | None = None) -> None:
        self.cfg = cfg or Config()
        self.index = VigilanceIndex(self.cfg)
        self._filter = StreamFilter(self.cfg)
        self._buffer = np.zeros((len(CHANNELS), 0))
        self._consumed = 0
        self._pushed = 0
        self.blinks = BlinkDetector(self.cfg)
        self.baseline = Baseline(Z_FEATURES, self.cfg.cal_windows)
        self._bad_before = 0       # windows starting before this sample are not trusted (stream glitch, filter restart)

    def reset_stream(self) -> None:
        """Restart the filters after a gap or corrupt samples; windows touching the restart are marked bad."""
        self._filter = StreamFilter(self.cfg)
        self.blinks.reset_filter()
        self._bad_before = self._pushed + int(round(self.cfg.settle_s * self.cfg.sfreq))

    def new_shift(self) -> None:
        """Start a new shift for the same, already calibrated person: full reserve, clock back to start."""
        self.index = VigilanceIndex(self.cfg)

    def recalibrate(self) -> None:
        """Start a new personal baseline from the next clean windows."""
        self.baseline = Baseline(Z_FEATURES, self.cfg.cal_windows)
        self.index.reset_detection()

    def push(self, eeg_volts: np.ndarray) -> list[dict]:
        """Feed raw samples, shape (8, n) in volts; returns the windows completed by them."""
        chunk = np.asarray(eeg_volts, dtype=float) * 1e6
        if chunk.ndim != 2 or chunk.shape[0] != len(CHANNELS):
            raise ValueError(f"Expected shape ({len(CHANNELS)}, n), got {chunk.shape}")
        corrupt = not np.isfinite(chunk).all()
        if corrupt:
            # NaN or Inf would poison the filter state for good, so zero them and restart the filters afterwards.
            chunk = np.nan_to_num(chunk, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        filtered = self._filter(chunk)
        self.blinks.push(filtered, self._pushed)
        self._pushed += filtered.shape[1]
        self._buffer = np.concatenate([self._buffer, filtered], axis=1)
        if corrupt:
            self.reset_stream()

        cfg = self.cfg
        results = []
        while self._buffer.shape[1] >= cfg.win_samples:
            t_end = (self._consumed + cfg.win_samples) / cfg.sfreq
            if t_end >= cfg.settle_s:
                results.append(self._process(self._buffer[:, : cfg.win_samples], t_end, untrusted=self._consumed < self._bad_before))
            self._buffer = self._buffer[:, cfg.step_samples :]
            self._consumed += cfg.step_samples
        return results

    def _process(self, window: np.ndarray, t_end: float, untrusted: bool = False) -> dict:
        features = window_features(window, self.cfg.sfreq)
        ptp = features["ptp_uv"]
        # Too large (movement), too flat (headset off, frozen stream), not a number, or right after a stream glitch.
        bad = untrusted or not np.isfinite(ptp) or ptp > self.cfg.reject_uv or ptp < self.cfg.flat_uv
        if not bad:
            self.baseline.add(features)
        calibrated = self.baseline.ready
        z = self.baseline.z(features) if calibrated and not bad else None
        result = {
            "t": round(t_end, 3),
            "bad": bool(bad),
            "calibrated": calibrated,
            "calibration": round(self.baseline.progress, 3),
            "features": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in features.items()},
            "z": None if z is None else {k: round(v, 3) for k, v in z.items()},
        }
        result.update(self.index.update(
            t_end, z, bool(bad), calibrated,
            last_blink=self.blinks.last_blink(), blinks_ready=self.blinks.ready,
        ))
        rate = self.blinks.rate_per_min(t_end)
        result["blinks_per_min"] = None if rate is None else round(rate, 1)
        return result


def run_offline(eeg_volts: np.ndarray, cfg: Config | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    """Run a whole recording through the pipeline, one second at a time."""
    pipeline = Pipeline(cfg)
    step = pipeline.cfg.step_samples
    results: list[dict] = []
    for start in range(0, eeg_volts.shape[1], step):
        results.extend(pipeline.push(eeg_volts[:, start : start + step]))
    return results
```

<!doctype html>
<html lang="pl">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Wachta — test czasu reakcji (PVT-B)</title>
<style>
  :root { color-scheme: dark; }
  body { margin: 0; min-height: 100vh; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
         background: #111; color: #eee; font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif; }

             font-size: 18vmin; color: #ff3b30; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  #counter.feedback { color: #ffd60a; }
  #counter.warning { color: #8e8e93; }

  a { color: #64d2ff; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="panel" id="setup">
  <h1>Test czasu reakcji (PVT-B, 3 min)</h1>
  <p>Patrz na środek ekranu. Gdy pojawi się <b>czerwony licznik</b>, jak najszybciej naciśnij
     <b>spację</b> (albo dotknij ekranu). Nie naciskaj, zanim licznik się pojawi.</p>
  <label for="person">Uczestnik</label>
  <input id="person" autocomplete="off">
  <label for="checkpoint">Punkt pomiarowy (T0 / T1 / T2)</label>
  <input id="checkpoint" autocomplete="off">
  <button id="start" type="button">Start</button>
  <p class="muted">Wyłącz powiadomienia i usiądź wygodnie. Przerwanie testu bez zapisu: klawisz Esc.</p>
  <p class="error" id="setup-error"></p>
</div>

<div id="arena"><div id="counter"></div><div id="progress"></div></div>

<div class="panel" id="result" hidden>
  <h1>Wynik</h1>
  <table id="summary"></table>
  <p class="muted" id="save-status"></p>
  <p><a id="download" hidden>Pobierz surowe dane (JSON)</a></p>
  <button id="again" type="button">Nowy test</button>
</div>

<script>
/*__PVT_CONFIG__*/
(function () {
  "use strict";

  const cfg = Object.assign({
    duration_s: 180, isi_min_s: 1, isi_max_s: 4, feedback_s: 1,
    lapse_ms: 355, false_start_ms: 100, timeout_ms: 30000,
    person: "", checkpoint: "", save_url: "/api/result"
  }, window.PVT_CONFIG || {});

  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  const setup = $("setup"), arena = $("arena"), counter = $("counter"), progress = $("progress");
  const result = $("result"), summary = $("summary"), saveStatus = $("save-status"), download = $("download");

  const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  $("person").value = params.get("person") || cfg.person || "";
  $("checkpoint").value = params.get("checkpoint") || cfg.checkpoint || "";

  // state: idle | waiting | pending | stimulus | feedback | done
  let state = "idle";
  let rts = [], anticipations = 0, testEnd = 0, onset = 0;
  let timer = null, rafId = null, progressTimer = null, testStart = 0;
  let meta = {};

  const now = () => performance.now();

  function eventTime(ev) {
    // Event timestamps share the performance.now() clock in current browsers; fall back otherwise.
    const t = ev && ev.timeStamp;
    const n = now();
    return (t > 0 && Math.abs(n - t) < 5000) ? t : n;
  }

  function clearTimers() {
    clearTimeout(timer); timer = null;
    if (rafId !== null) { cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = null; }
  }

  function randomWaitMs() {
    const lo = Math.max(0, cfg.isi_min_s - cfg.feedback_s);
    const hi = Math.max(lo, cfg.isi_max_s - cfg.feedback_s);
    return (lo + Math.random() * (hi - lo)) * 1000;
  }

  function scheduleNext() {
    clearTimers();
    counter.textContent = ""; counter.className = "";
    const remaining = testEnd - now();
    if (remaining <= 0) { finish(); return; }
    state = "waiting";
    const waitMs = randomWaitMs();
    if (waitMs >= remaining) {
      timer = setTimeout(finish, remaining + 5);
      return;
    }
    timer = setTimeout(() => (now() >= testEnd ? finish() : showStimulus()), waitMs);
  }

  function showStimulus() {
    state = "pending";
    rafId = requestAnimationFrame((frameTime) => {
      counter.className = ""; counter.textContent = "0";
      onset = frameTime;
      state = "stimulus";
      tick();
    });
  }

  function tick() {
    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (state !== "stimulus") return;
      const elapsed = now() - onset;
      if (elapsed >= cfg.timeout_ms) { record(cfg.timeout_ms, "brak reakcji"); return; }
      counter.textContent = String(Math.floor(elapsed));
      tick();
    });
  }

  function showFeedback(text, cls) {
    state = "feedback";
    counter.className = cls; counter.textContent = text;
    timer = setTimeout(scheduleNext, cfg.feedback_s * 1000);
  }

  function record(rt, label) {
    clearTimers();
    rts.push(Math.round(rt * 10) / 10);
    showFeedback(label || String(Math.round(rt)), "feedback");
  }

  function respond(ev) {
    const t = eventTime(ev);
    if (state === "stimulus") {
      record(Math.max(0, t - onset));
    } else if (state === "waiting" || state === "pending") {
      anticipations += 1;
      clearTimers();
      showFeedback("FS", "warning");
    } else if (state === "feedback") {
      anticipations += 1;
    }
  }

  function start() {
    const person = $("person").value.trim();
    const checkpoint = $("checkpoint").value.trim();
    if (!person || !checkpoint) {
      $("setup-error").textContent = "Wpisz uczestnika i punkt pomiarowy.";
      return;
    }
    if (/[\\/\r\n]/.test(person)) {
      $("setup-error").textContent = "Imię uczestnika nie może zawierać ukośników ani nowych linii.";
      return;
    }
    $("setup-error").textContent = "";
    meta = { person, checkpoint, started_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    rts = []; anticipations = 0;
    setup.hidden = true; result.hidden = true;
    arena.style.display = "flex";
    testStart = now();
    testEnd = testStart + cfg.duration_s * 1000;
    progressTimer = setInterval(() => {
      progress.style.width = Math.min(100, (now() - testStart) / (cfg.duration_s * 10)) + "%";
    }, 250);
    scheduleNext();
  }

  function stopArena() {
    clearTimers();
    clearInterval(progressTimer);
    arena.style.display = "none";
    progress.style.width = "0";
  }

  function abort() {
    stopArena();
    state = "idle";
    setup.hidden = false;
  }

  function addRow(label, value) {
    const tr = document.createElement("tr");
    const a = document.createElement("td"); a.textContent = label;
    const b = document.createElement("td"); b.textContent = (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") ? "—" : value;
    tr.append(a, b); summary.append(tr);
  }

  function showSummary(row) {
    summary.textContent = "";
    addRow("Uczestnik / punkt", row.person + " / " + row.checkpoint);
    addRow("Bodźce", row.n_stimuli);
    addRow("Mediana czasu reakcji [ms]", row.median_rt_ms);
    addRow("Średni czas reakcji [ms]", row.mean_rt_ms);
    addRow("Szybkość reakcji 1/RT [1/s]", row.mean_speed);
    addRow("Lapsy (≥ " + cfg.lapse_ms + " ms)", row.lapses);
    addRow("Falstarty", row.false_starts);
    addRow("Wynik (performance score)", row.performance_score);
  }

  let downloadUrl = null;

  function offerDownload(payload) {
    if (downloadUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(downloadUrl);
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
    downloadUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    download.href = downloadUrl;
    download.download = "pvt-" + payload.person + "-" + payload.checkpoint + ".json";
    download.hidden = false;
  }

  async function finish() {
    if (state === "done") return;
    state = "done";
    stopArena();
    result.hidden = false;
    download.hidden = true;
    summary.textContent = "";
    saveStatus.textContent = "Zapisywanie…";
    const payload = Object.assign({}, meta, {
      duration_s: cfg.duration_s, rts_ms: rts, anticipations: anticipations
    });
    try {
      const response = await fetch(cfg.save_url, {
        method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || !data.ok) throw new Error(data.error || ("HTTP " + response.status));
      showSummary(data.result);
      saveStatus.textContent = "Zapisano do " + data.saved_to + ".";
    } catch (err) {
      saveStatus.textContent = "Nie udało się zapisać wyniku (" + err.message + "). Pobierz surowe dane i wczytaj je: " +
        "python -m wachta.pvt --import <plik.json>";
      offerDownload(payload);
    }
  }

  $("start").addEventListener("click", start);
  $("again").addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (downloadUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(downloadUrl); downloadUrl = null; }
    result.hidden = true; setup.hidden = false; state = "idle";
  });

  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (arena.style.display !== "flex") return;
    if (ev.code === "Escape") { ev.preventDefault(); abort(); return; }
    if (ev.code === "Space" || ev.key === " ") {
      ev.preventDefault();
      if (!ev.repeat) respond(ev);
    }
  });
  arena.addEventListener("pointerdown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.pointerType === "mouse" && ev.button !== 0) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    respond(ev);
  });
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/pvt.py`
```
"""Brief psychomotor vigilance test (PVT-B) in the browser.

A 3-minute reaction time test used as a behavioural vigilance label next to KSS at
checkpoints. Parameters follow Basner, Mollicone & Dinges (2011), Acta Astronautica
69:949-959 (PMC3197786):

* test duration 3 min;
* response-to-stimulus interval 1-4 s, including 1 s of reaction time feedback;
* lapse: reaction time >= 355 ms (PVT-B threshold; 500 ms is used for the 10-min PVT);
* false start: response without a stimulus or reaction time < 100 ms;
* no response within 30 s counts as a lapse with a 30 s reaction time.

Run ``python -m wachta.pvt --person wojtek --checkpoint T1``. The command starts a
local web server (127.0.0.1 only), opens the test page and appends one row per test
to ``data/recordings/pvt.csv``. Scoring happens here, in Python, so the browser page
only measures and sends raw reaction times.

Browser timing adds a roughly constant delay (display and keyboard latency, typically
tens of ms). Results are comparable within one person on one computer, not with
published absolute norms.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from http import HTTPStatus
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
import csv
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import statistics
import sys
import threading
from typing import Any, Sequence
from urllib.parse import urlencode, urlsplit
import webbrowser

from .session import normalize_checkpoint, normalize_person

DEFAULT_RESULTS_FILE = Path("data/recordings/pvt.csv")
DEFAULT_PORT = 8765
PAGE_PATH = Path(__file__).with_name("pvt.html")
CONFIG_PLACEHOLDER = "/*__PVT_CONFIG__*/"
MAX_BODY_BYTES = 256 * 1024
MAX_RESPONSES = 2000

PVT_FIELDS = (
    "person",
    "checkpoint",
    "started_at",
    "duration_s",
    "n_stimuli",
    "false_starts",
    "lapses",
    "mean_rt_ms",
    "median_rt_ms",
    "mean_speed",
    "slowest10_speed",
    "performance_score",
    "rts_ms",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PvtConfig:
    duration_s: float = 180.0
    isi_min_s: float = 1.0      # response-to-stimulus interval, feedback included
    isi_max_s: float = 4.0
    feedback_s: float = 1.0
    lapse_ms: float = 355.0
    false_start_ms: float = 100.0
    timeout_ms: float = 30000.0


def _mean(values: Sequence[float]) -> float | None:
    return sum(values) / len(values) if values else None


def _round(value: float | None, digits: int) -> float | None:
    return None if value is None else round(value, digits)


def score_pvt(
    rts_ms: Sequence[float],
    anticipations: int = 0,
    cfg: PvtConfig = PvtConfig(),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Score one PVT run.

    Parameters
    ----------
    rts_ms : reaction times to stimuli in ms, one per stimulus; a timeout is ``cfg.timeout_ms``.
    anticipations : responses made while no stimulus was shown.
    """
    rts = [float(r) for r in rts_ms]
    valid = [r for r in rts if r >= cfg.false_start_ms]
    false_starts = int(anticipations) + (len(rts) - len(valid))
    lapses = sum(1 for r in valid if r >= cfg.lapse_ms)
    speeds = sorted(1000.0 / r for r in valid)
    n_slowest = max(1, math.ceil(0.1 * len(speeds))) if speeds else 0

    # Basner & Dinges (2011): 1 - (lapses + false starts) / valid stimuli including false starts.
    denominator = len(rts) + int(anticipations)
    performance = 1.0 - (lapses + false_starts) / denominator if denominator else None

    return {
        "n_stimuli": len(rts),
        "false_starts": false_starts,
        "lapses": lapses,
        "mean_rt_ms": _round(_mean(valid), 1),
        "median_rt_ms": _round(statistics.median(valid) if valid else None, 1),
        "mean_speed": _round(_mean(speeds), 3),
        "slowest10_speed": _round(_mean(speeds[:n_slowest]), 3),
        "performance_score": _round(performance, 3),
    }


def _parse_started_at(value: Any) -> str:
    if value in (None, ""):
        return datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    raw = str(value).strip()
    if len(raw) > 64:
        raise ValueError("started_at is too long")
    if raw.endswith("Z"):
        raw = raw[:-1] + "+00:00"
    try:
        parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"started_at must be an ISO-8601 timestamp, got {value!r}") from exc
    if parsed.tzinfo is None:
        parsed = parsed.astimezone()
    return parsed.astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")


def _parse_rts(value: Any, cfg: PvtConfig) -> list[float]:
    if not isinstance(value, list):
        raise ValueError("rts_ms must be a list of numbers")
    if len(value) > MAX_RESPONSES:
        raise ValueError(f"too many responses ({len(value)})")
    rts = []
    for item in value:
        if isinstance(item, bool) or not isinstance(item, (int, float)):
            raise ValueError(f"reaction time must be a number, got {item!r}")
        rt = float(item)
        if not math.isfinite(rt) or rt < 0 or rt > cfg.timeout_ms:
            raise ValueError(f"reaction time out of range: {item!r}")
        rts.append(round(rt, 1))
    return rts


def _parse_count(value: Any, name: str) -> int:
    if value in (None, ""):
        return 0
    if (
        isinstance(value, bool)
        or not isinstance(value, (int, float))
        or not math.isfinite(value)
        or value != int(value)
    ):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a non-negative integer, got {value!r}")
    count = int(value)
    if not 0 <= count <= MAX_RESPONSES:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} out of range: {count}")
    return count


def build_row(payload: dict[str, Any], cfg: PvtConfig = PvtConfig()) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate a result sent by the browser and turn it into a ``pvt.csv`` row."""
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise ValueError("result must be a JSON object")
    raw_person = payload.get("person")
    raw_checkpoint = payload.get("checkpoint")
    if not isinstance(raw_person, str) or not isinstance(raw_checkpoint, str):
        raise ValueError("person and checkpoint must be strings")
    person = normalize_person(raw_person)
    checkpoint = normalize_checkpoint(raw_checkpoint)
    rts = _parse_rts(payload.get("rts_ms"), cfg)
    anticipations = _parse_count(payload.get("anticipations"), "anticipations")

    duration = payload.get("duration_s", cfg.duration_s)
    if isinstance(duration, bool) or not isinstance(duration, (int, float)) or not 1 <= duration <= 3600:
        raise ValueError(f"duration_s out of range: {duration!r}")

    metrics = score_pvt(rts, anticipations, cfg)
    row: dict[str, Any] = {
        "person": person,
        "checkpoint": checkpoint,
        "started_at": _parse_started_at(payload.get("started_at")),
        "duration_s": round(float(duration), 1),
        **metrics,
        "rts_ms": ";".join(f"{r:g}" for r in rts),
    }
    return row


def append_result(results_path: Path | str, row: dict[str, Any]) -> Path:
    """Create or append one row to ``pvt.csv`` (header checked against ``PVT_FIELDS``)."""
    path = Path(results_path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    needs_header = not path.exists() or path.stat().st_size == 0
    if not needs_header:
        with path.open("r", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as handle:
            header = next(csv.reader(handle), None)
        if header is None:
            needs_header = True
        elif tuple(header) != PVT_FIELDS:
            raise ValueError(f"{path} has unexpected columns {header!r}; expected {list(PVT_FIELDS)!r}")

    with path.open("w" if needs_header else "a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=PVT_FIELDS, lineterminator="\n")
        if needs_header:
            writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerow({field: "" if row.get(field) is None else row.get(field) for field in PVT_FIELDS})
    return path


def render_page(cfg: PvtConfig, person: str = "", checkpoint: str = "") -> str:
    """Return the test page with the configuration embedded."""
    page = PAGE_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    settings = {**asdict(cfg), "person": person, "checkpoint": checkpoint, "save_url": "/api/result"}
    script = "window.PVT_CONFIG = " + json.dumps(settings, ensure_ascii=False).replace("</", "<\\/") + ";"
    return page.replace(CONFIG_PLACEHOLDER, script, 1)


class PvtServer(ThreadingHTTPServer):
    daemon_threads = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        address: tuple[str, int],
        results_path: Path | str = DEFAULT_RESULTS_FILE,
        cfg: PvtConfig = PvtConfig(),
        person: str = "",
        checkpoint: str = "",
        once: bool = False,
        out=sys.stdout,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(address, PvtRequestHandler)
        self.results_path = Path(results_path)
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.person = person
        self.checkpoint = checkpoint
        self.once = once
        self.out = out
        self.saved_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def save(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = build_row(payload, self.cfg)
        with self._lock:
            append_result(self.results_path, row)
            self.saved_rows.append(row)
        self.out.write(
            f"Zapisano PVT: {row['person']} {row['checkpoint']} | lapsy {row['lapses']}, "
            f"falstarty {row['false_starts']}, mediana {row['median_rt_ms']} ms -> {self.results_path}\n"
        )
        self.out.flush()
        if self.once:
            threading.Thread(target=self.shutdown, daemon=True).start()
        return row


class PvtRequestHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server: PvtServer

    def log_message(self, format: str, *args: Any) -> None:  # noqa: A002 - stdlib signature
        return

    def _send(self, status: HTTPStatus, body: bytes, content_type: str) -> None:
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _send_json(self, status: HTTPStatus, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self._send(status, json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"), "application/json; charset=utf-8")

    def do_GET(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802 - stdlib naming
        path = urlsplit(self.path).path
        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            page = render_page(self.server.cfg, self.server.person, self.server.checkpoint)
            self._send(HTTPStatus.OK, page.encode("utf-8"), "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        elif path == "/favicon.ico":
            self._send(HTTPStatus.NO_CONTENT, b"", "image/x-icon")
        else:
            self._send_json(HTTPStatus.NOT_FOUND, {"ok": False, "error": "not found"})

    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802 - stdlib naming
        if urlsplit(self.path).path != "/api/result":
            self._send_json(HTTPStatus.NOT_FOUND, {"ok": False, "error": "not found"})
            return
        try:
            length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
        except ValueError:
            length = -1
        if not 0 < length <= MAX_BODY_BYTES:
            self._send_json(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, {"ok": False, "error": "invalid body size"})
            return
        try:
            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length).decode("utf-8"))
            row = self.server.save(payload)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            self._send_json(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, {"ok": False, "error": str(exc)})
            return
        self._send_json(HTTPStatus.OK, {"ok": True, "result": row, "saved_to": str(self.server.results_path)})


def import_result(json_path: Path | str, results_path: Path | str, cfg: PvtConfig = PvtConfig()) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Score and save a raw result downloaded from the page when no server was running."""
    payload = json.loads(Path(json_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    row = build_row(payload, cfg)
    append_result(results_path, row)
    return row


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="python -m wachta.pvt",
        description="Test czasu reakcji PVT-B (3 min) w przeglądarce; wynik trafia do pvt.csv.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--person", default="", help="Imię uczestnika (wpisane z góry na stronie testu)")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint", default="", help="Punkt pomiarowy, np. T0, T1, T2")
    parser.add_argument("--results-file", default=str(DEFAULT_RESULTS_FILE), help="Plik wyników (domyślnie: data/recordings/pvt.csv)")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PORT, help=f"Port lokalnego serwera (domyślnie: {DEFAULT_PORT})")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=PvtConfig.duration_s, help="Czas testu w sekundach (domyślnie: 180)")
    parser.add_argument("--no-browser", action="store_true", help="Nie otwieraj przeglądarki automatycznie")
    parser.add_argument("--once", action="store_true", help="Zakończ po zapisaniu pierwszego wyniku")
    parser.add_argument("--import", dest="import_json", metavar="PLIK_JSON", help="Wczytaj surowy wynik pobrany ze strony testu i zapisz go do pliku wyników")
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    if not 10 <= args.duration <= 3600:
        sys.stderr.write("Błąd: --duration musi mieć od 10 do 3600 s\n")
        return 2
    cfg = PvtConfig(duration_s=float(args.duration))

    if args.import_json:
        try:
            row = import_result(args.import_json, args.results_file, cfg)
        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
            sys.stderr.write(f"Błąd: {exc}\n")
            return 2
        print(f"Zapisano wynik {row['person']} {row['checkpoint']} do {args.results_file}")
        return 0

    try:
        person = normalize_person(args.person) if args.person else ""
        checkpoint = normalize_checkpoint(args.checkpoint) if args.checkpoint else ""
        server = PvtServer(("127.0.0.1", args.port), args.results_file, cfg, person, checkpoint, args.once)
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        sys.stderr.write(f"Błąd: {exc}\n")
        return 2

    query = urlencode({k: v for k, v in (("person", person), ("checkpoint", checkpoint)) if v})
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}/" + (f"?{query}" if query else "")
    print(f"Test PVT-B: {url}\nWyniki: {args.results_file}\nZakończ: Ctrl+C", flush=True)
    if not args.no_browser:
        webbrowser.open(url)
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        server.server_close()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/recordings.py`
```
"""Loading team recordings (``data/recordings``) and their protocol annotations."""

import csv
from pathlib import Path
from typing import NamedTuple

import numpy as np

from .config import CHANNELS

DEFAULT_DIR = Path("data/recordings")


class Block(NamedTuple):
    onset: float
    duration: float
    label: str

    @property
    def end(self) -> float:
        return self.onset + self.duration


def find_recordings(folder: Path | str = DEFAULT_DIR) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """Map the task name embedded in each file name (e.g. ``filip``) to its ``.fif`` path."""
    found = {}
    for path in sorted(Path(folder).glob("*-raw.fif")):
        name = path.name.split("task-")[1].split("_")[0] if "task-" in path.name else path.stem
        found[name] = path
    return found


def load_eeg(path: Path | str) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float]:
    """Return the 8 EEG channels in volts, ordered as ``CHANNELS``, and the sampling rate."""
    import mne

    raw = mne.io.read_raw_fif(path, preload=True, verbose="ERROR")
    missing = [ch for ch in CHANNELS if ch not in raw.ch_names]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing channels {missing}")
    return raw.get_data(picks=list(CHANNELS)), float(raw.info["sfreq"])


def annotations_path(fif_path: Path | str) -> Path:
    fif_path = Path(fif_path)
    return fif_path.with_name(fif_path.name.replace("-raw.fif", "-raw_annotations.csv"))


def load_annotations(fif_path: Path | str) -> list[Block]:
    """Read protocol blocks stored next to a recording; empty list if there are none."""
    path = annotations_path(fif_path)
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return [
            Block(float(row["onset"]), float(row["duration"]), row["description"])
            for row in csv.DictReader(handle)
            if row.get("duration")
        ]
```
