## FILE: `src/wachta/server.py`
```
"""Web server: runs the pipeline on each source and streams results to the dashboard.

Examples:
    python -m wachta.server --file filip --start 540 --team rudy:230 adi:240
    python -m wachta.server --file filip --team rudy:380-520 adi:370-500   # team members loop over a calm segment
    python -m wachta.server --source board --team rudy:230 adi:240

The first source is the worker shown in the main view. ``--team`` adds replayed
recordings so the supervisor panel has more than one row.
"""

import argparse
import asyncio
import contextlib
import dataclasses
import logging
import time
from collections import deque
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from .config import Config
from .pipeline import Pipeline
from .recordings import find_recordings
from .sources import BoardSource, ReplaySource, resolve_recording

log = logging.getLogger("wachta")

WEB_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "web"
RETRY_S = 3.0
HISTORY_S = 900            # seconds of per-window history kept for new clients
TRACE_EVERY = 5            # keep every n-th window in the long reserve trace

# Fields of a pipeline result that the dashboard needs.
CLIENT_FIELDS = (
    "t", "state", "reserve", "fast", "slow", "quality", "lapse", "new_lapse", "lapses_recent",
    "lapse_count", "calibration", "minutes_to_warn", "minutes_to_stop", "work_min",
    "drain_per_hour", "bad", "on_break", "lapse_kind", "lapses_eyes_closed", "lapses_eyes_open",
    "eye_state", "blinks_per_min", "probes_done", "probe_lapses", "last_rt_ms", "context_factor",
    "clock_h", "awake_h", "probe_threshold_ms", "probe_failed_recently",
)


def _client_entry(result: dict) -> dict:
    entry = {key: result[key] for key in CLIENT_FIELDS}
    for key in ("minutes_to_warn", "minutes_to_stop"):
        if entry[key] is not None:
            entry[key] = round(entry[key], 1)
    return entry


class Worker:
    """One person: a source of samples, a pipeline and the recent results."""

    def __init__(self, worker_id: str, source, cfg: Config, primary: bool = False) -> None:
        self.id = worker_id
        self.primary = primary
        self.source = source
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.pipeline = Pipeline(cfg)
        self.history: deque[dict] = deque(maxlen=HISTORY_S)
        self.trace: deque[list[float]] = deque(maxlen=4000)   # [work_min, reserve]
        self.error: str | None = None
        self._windows = 0
        self.pending_probe: int | None = None
        self._probe_seq = 0
        self._probe_sent = 0.0
        self.last_t = 0.0

    def new_probe(self) -> int:
        self._probe_seq += 1
        self.pending_probe = self._probe_seq
        self._probe_sent = time.monotonic()
        return self._probe_seq

    def drop_stale_probe(self) -> None:
        """Forget a probe nobody answered (e.g. the page was closed); it does not count as a lapse."""
        if self.pending_probe is not None and time.monotonic() - self._probe_sent > self.cfg.probe_timeout_ms / 1000 + 5:
            self.pending_probe = None

    def probe_response(self, probe_id, rt_ms) -> bool:
        """Accept the first answer to the pending probe; later or stale answers are ignored."""
        if probe_id != self.pending_probe:
            return False
        self.pending_probe = None
        try:
            value = None if rt_ms is None else float(rt_ms)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return False                      # garbage from the page: forget the probe, count nothing
        self.pipeline.index.record_probe(self.last_t, value)
        return True

    def record(self, result: dict) -> dict:
        entry = _client_entry(result)
        self.last_t = entry["t"]
        self.history.append(entry)
        if entry["state"] != "calibrating" and self._windows % TRACE_EVERY == 0:
            self.trace.append([entry["work_min"], entry["reserve"]])
        self._windows += 1
        return entry

    def handle(self, action: str) -> None:
        if action == "probe_now":
            self.pipeline.index.request_probe()
        elif action == "break_start":
            self.pipeline.index.start_break()
        elif action == "break_end":
            self.pipeline.index.end_break()
        elif action == "recalibrate":
            self.pipeline.recalibrate()
            self.pending_probe = None
        elif action == "reset":
            # New shift, same person: keep the calibration and blink threshold.
            self.pipeline.new_shift()
            self.pending_probe = None
            self.history.clear()
            self.trace.clear()

    def describe(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "name": self.source.name,
            "live": self.source.live,
            "error": self.error,
            "history": list(self.history),
            "trace": list(self.trace),
        }


class Hub:
    """Connected dashboards."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.clients: set[WebSocket] = set()

    async def broadcast(self, message: dict) -> None:
        for client in list(self.clients):
            try:
                await client.send_json(message)
            except Exception:
                self.clients.discard(client)


async def _run_worker(worker: Worker, hub: Hub) -> None:
    """Pump one source through its pipeline; live sources reconnect after errors."""
    while True:
        try:
            async for chunk in worker.source.chunks():
                if worker.error:
                    worker.error = None
                    await hub.broadcast({"type": "status", "worker": worker.id, "error": None})
                for result in worker.pipeline.push(chunk):
                    await hub.broadcast({"type": "update", "worker": worker.id, "data": worker.record(result)})
                    # Probes go to the person in the main view only, and only if someone can answer.
                    worker.drop_stale_probe()
                    if result["probe"] and worker.primary and hub.clients and worker.pending_probe is None:
                        await hub.broadcast({"type": "probe", "worker": worker.id, "id": worker.new_probe(),
                                             "timeout_ms": worker.cfg.probe_timeout_ms,
                                             "threshold_ms": result["probe_threshold_ms"],
                                             "reason": result["probe_reason"]})
            return
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            raise
        except Exception as error:
            worker.error = str(error)
            worker.pipeline.reset_stream()      # the stream has a gap now; do not trust windows across it
            log.warning("%s: %s", worker.source.name, error)
            await hub.broadcast({"type": "status", "worker": worker.id, "error": worker.error})
            if not worker.source.live:
                return
            await asyncio.sleep(RETRY_S)


def create_app(workers: list[Worker], cfg: Config) -> FastAPI:
    if not workers:
        raise ValueError("At least one worker is required")
    hub = Hub()
    by_id = {worker.id: worker for worker in workers}

    @contextlib.asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):
        tasks = [asyncio.create_task(_run_worker(worker, hub)) for worker in workers]
        yield
        for task in tasks:
            task.cancel()
        await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)

    app = FastAPI(title="Wachta", lifespan=lifespan)

    def hello() -> dict:
        return {
            "type": "hello",
            "primary": workers[0].id,
            "config": {
                "z_enter": cfg.z_enter,
                "warn_below": cfg.warn_below,
                "stop_below": cfg.stop_below,
                "time_scale": cfg.time_scale,
                "step_s": cfg.step_s,
                "drain_per_hour": cfg.drain_per_hour,
                "probe_lapse_ms": cfg.probe_lapse_ms,
                "probe_timeout_ms": cfg.probe_timeout_ms,
                "probe_interval_s": cfg.probe_interval_s,
            },
            "workers": [worker.describe() for worker in workers],
        }

    @app.get("/api/state")
    def state() -> dict:
        """Latest result per worker, for scripts and quick checks."""
        return {
            worker.id: {"name": worker.source.name, "error": worker.error,
                        "latest": worker.history[-1] if worker.history else None}
            for worker in workers
        }

    @app.websocket("/ws")
    async def stream(websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
        await websocket.accept()
        await websocket.send_json(hello())
        hub.clients.add(websocket)
        try:
            while True:
                message = await websocket.receive_json()
                worker = by_id.get(message.get("worker"), workers[0])
                if message.get("action") == "probe_response":
                    worker.probe_response(message.get("id"), message.get("rt_ms"))
                    continue
                if message.get("action") == "reset":
                    # "Nowa zmiana" starts a new shift for the whole team at once, calibrations kept.
                    for member in workers:
                        member.handle("reset")
                    await hub.broadcast(hello())
                    continue
                worker.handle(str(message.get("action")))
        except WebSocketDisconnect:
            pass
        finally:
            hub.clients.discard(websocket)

    app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=WEB_DIR, html=True), name="web")
    return app


def _replay_worker(worker_id: str, spec: str, cfg: Config, speed: float, start_s: float) -> Worker:
    """Build a replayed worker from ``name``, ``name:start`` or ``name:start-end`` (seconds, end loops back to start)."""
    name, _, offset = spec.partition(":")
    offset, _, end = offset.partition("-")
    path = resolve_recording(name)
    label = path.name.split("task-")[1].split("_")[0] if "task-" in path.name else path.stem
    source = ReplaySource.from_file(
        path, name=f"{label.capitalize()} (nagranie)", speed=speed, start_s=float(offset) if offset else start_s,
        end_s=float(end) if end else None,
    )
    return Worker(worker_id, source, cfg)


def build_workers(args: argparse.Namespace, cfg: Config) -> list[Worker]:
    if args.source == "board":
        workers = [Worker("w0", BoardSource(name=args.name or "Na żywo"), cfg, primary=True)]
    else:
        spec = args.file or next(iter(find_recordings()), None)
        if spec is None:
            raise SystemExit("Brak nagrań w data/recordings. Podaj --file albo użyj --source board.")
        workers = [_replay_worker("w0", spec, cfg, args.speed, args.start)]
        workers[0].primary = True
        if args.name:
            workers[0].source.name = args.name
    for i, spec in enumerate(args.team, start=1):
        workers.append(_replay_worker(f"w{i}", spec, cfg, args.speed, 0.0))
    return workers


def _hours(text: str) -> float:
    hours, _, minutes = text.partition(":")
    value = int(hours) + int(minutes or 0) / 60.0
    if not 0 <= value < 24:
        raise ValueError(f"Niepoprawna godzina: {text}")
    return value


def context_settings(wake: str | None, clock: str | None) -> dict:
    """Config fields for the context model from ``--wake`` and ``--clock`` (HH:MM)."""
    if wake is None and clock is None:
        return {}
    start = _hours(clock) if clock else datetime.now().hour + datetime.now().minute / 60.0
    settings = {"start_clock_h": start}
    if wake is not None:
        settings["awake_at_start_h"] = (start - _hours(wake)) % 24.0
    return settings


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Wachta dashboard server")
    parser.add_argument("--source", choices=("replay", "board"), default="replay",
                        help="replay a recording (default) or read BrainAccess Board live")
    parser.add_argument("--file", help="recording to replay: a .fif path or a team recording name, e.g. filip")
    parser.add_argument("--start", type=float, default=0.0, help="replay start offset in seconds")
    parser.add_argument("--speed", type=float, default=1.0, help="replay speed, 1 is real time")
    parser.add_argument("--name", help="display name of the main worker")
    parser.add_argument("--team", nargs="*", default=[], metavar="NAME[:START[-END]]",
                        help="extra replayed recordings for the supervisor panel")
    parser.add_argument("--time-scale", type=float, default=60.0,
                        help="shift seconds per real second; 60 means one minute of demo is one hour of work")
    parser.add_argument("--wake", metavar="HH:MM", help="wake-up time of the main worker; turns on the context model")
    parser.add_argument("--clock", metavar="HH:MM", help="time of day at shift start (default: now); use with --wake")
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8000)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    import uvicorn

    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(message)s")
    try:
        cfg = dataclasses.replace(Config(), time_scale=args.time_scale, **context_settings(args.wake, args.clock))
        workers = build_workers(args, cfg)
    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as error:
        parser.error(str(error))               # a readable message instead of a traceback
    log.info("Wachta: http://%s:%s  (%s)", args.host, args.port, ", ".join(w.source.name for w in workers))
    uvicorn.run(create_app(workers, cfg), host=args.host, port=args.port, log_level="warning")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## FILE: `src/wachta/session.py`
```
"""Checkpoint protocol runner and metadata logger for team EEG sessions.

Guides a participant through the fixed 3-minute EEG checkpoint protocol
(``eyes_open``, ``eyes_closed``, ``math``) followed by KSS (1-9) and optional
Garmin Venu 2 readings, then writes:

* ``<recording>-raw_annotations.csv`` in MNE-compatible ``onset,duration,description``
  format next to the ``.fif`` recording;
* a row in ``data/recordings/checkpoints.csv``.

Run with ``python -m wachta.session``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
import math
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time
from typing import Callable, Sequence, TextIO

DEFAULT_RECORDINGS_DIR = Path("data/recordings")
DEFAULT_STEP_SECONDS = 60.0
VALID_CHECKPOINTS = ("T0", "T1", "T2", "N1", "N2")   # N*: night checkpoints

CHECKPOINT_FIELDS = (
    "person",
    "checkpoint",
    "started_at",
    "kss",
    "body_battery",
    "stress",
    "heart_rate",
    "hrv_ms",
    "recording_file",
    "notes",
)

KSS_GUIDE = (
    "Skala senności KSS (1–9):\n"
    "  1 = skrajnie czujny  | 3 = czujny | 5 = ani czujny, ani senny\n"
    "  7 = senny, bez walki ze snem | 9 = bardzo senny, walka ze snem"
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProtocolStep:
    label: str
    duration_s: float
    instruction: str


DEFAULT_STEPS: tuple[ProtocolStep, ...] = (
    ProtocolStep(
        label="eyes_open",
        duration_s=DEFAULT_STEP_SECONDS,
        instruction="Oczy otwarte, patrz spokojnie w jeden punkt (kalibracja)",
    ),
    ProtocolStep(
        label="eyes_closed",
        duration_s=DEFAULT_STEP_SECONDS,
        instruction="Zamknij oczy, rozluźnij się (alfa potyliczna)",
    ),
    ProtocolStep(
        label="math",
        duration_s=DEFAULT_STEP_SECONDS,
        instruction="Liczenie w pamięci: odejmuj 7 od 1000 (oczy otwarte)",
    ),
)


MONOTONY_INSTRUCTION = "Monotonia: siedź spokojnie, oczy otwarte, patrz w jeden punkt, bez telefonu i rozmowy"


def build_steps(
    step_seconds: float = DEFAULT_STEP_SECONDS, monotony_minutes: float = 0.0
) -> tuple[ProtocolStep, ...]:
    """Return the fixed checkpoint steps, optionally followed by a monotony block."""
    if step_seconds <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"step_seconds must be positive, got {step_seconds}")
    if monotony_minutes < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"monotony_minutes cannot be negative, got {monotony_minutes}")
    steps = tuple(
        ProtocolStep(step.label, float(step_seconds), step.instruction)
        for step in DEFAULT_STEPS
    )
    if monotony_minutes > 0:
        steps += (ProtocolStep("monotony", float(monotony_minutes) * 60.0, MONOTONY_INSTRUCTION),)
    return steps


def normalize_person(value: str) -> str:
    cleaned = value.strip().lower()
    if not cleaned:
        raise ValueError("person cannot be empty")
    if any(sep in cleaned for sep in ("/", "\\", "\n", "\r")):
        raise ValueError(f"invalid person name: {value!r}")
    return cleaned


def normalize_checkpoint(value: str) -> str:
    cleaned = value.strip().upper()
    if not cleaned:
        raise ValueError("checkpoint cannot be empty")
    return cleaned


def parse_kss(value: str | int) -> int:
    raw = str(value).strip()
    try:
        score = int(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"KSS must be an integer 1..9, got {value!r}") from exc
    if not 1 <= score <= 9:
        raise ValueError(f"KSS must be between 1 and 9, got {score}")
    return score


def parse_optional_number(
    value: str | float | int | None,
    field_name: str,
    *,
    min_value: float | None = None,
    max_value: float | None = None,
    integer: bool = False,
) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return ""
    raw = str(value).strip()
    if not raw:
        return ""
    raw_normalized = raw.replace(",", ".")
    try:
        num = int(raw_normalized) if integer else float(raw_normalized)
    except ValueError as exc:
        kind = "integer" if integer else "number"
        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} must be a valid {kind}, got {value!r}") from exc
    if min_value is not None and num < min_value:
        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} must be >= {min_value}, got {num}")
    if max_value is not None and num > max_value:
        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} must be <= {max_value}, got {num}")
    if isinstance(num, float) and num.is_integer():
        return str(int(num))
    return str(num)


def annotations_path_for(recording_path: Path | str) -> Path:
    """Return the companion annotations CSV path for a ``.fif`` recording."""
    path = Path(recording_path)
    if path.name.endswith("-raw.fif"):
        return path.with_name(path.name.replace("-raw.fif", "-raw_annotations.csv"))
    return path.with_name(f"{path.stem}-raw_annotations.csv")


def write_annotations(
    path: Path | str,
    steps: Sequence[ProtocolStep] = DEFAULT_STEPS,
) -> Path:
    """Write ``onset,duration,description`` CSV for the checkpoint blocks."""
    out_path = Path(path)
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    onset = 0.0
    with out_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.writer(handle, lineterminator="\n")
        writer.writerow(["onset", "duration", "description"])
        for step in steps:
            writer.writerow([f"{onset:.3f}", f"{step.duration_s:.3f}", step.label])
            onset += step.duration_s
    return out_path


def find_candidate_recordings(
    recordings_dir: Path | str,
    person: str,
    checkpoint: str,
    *,
    started_epoch: float | None = None,
) -> list[Path]:
    """Return ``.fif`` files in ``recordings_dir`` ordered by relevance and recency."""
    folder = Path(recordings_dir)
    if not folder.exists():
        return []

    person_lc = person.lower()
    cp_lc = checkpoint.lower()
    task_token = f"{person_lc}-{cp_lc}"
    alt_token = f"{person_lc}_{cp_lc}"

    candidates: list[tuple[int, float, Path]] = []
    for path in folder.rglob("*.fif"):
        try:
            mtime = path.stat().st_mtime
        except OSError:
            continue
        name_lc = path.name.lower()
        score = 0
        if task_token in name_lc or alt_token in name_lc:
            score += 4
        elif person_lc in name_lc:
            score += 2
        if started_epoch is not None and mtime >= started_epoch - 5.0:
            score += 3
        candidates.append((score, mtime, path))

    candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1]), reverse=True)
    return [item[2] for item in candidates]


def format_recording_ref(recording_path: Path | str, recordings_dir: Path | str) -> str:
    """Format recording path relative to ``recordings_dir`` when located inside it."""
    rec = Path(recording_path)
    base = Path(recordings_dir)
    try:
        return rec.resolve().relative_to(base.resolve()).as_posix()
    except ValueError:
        return rec.as_posix()


def existing_checkpoints(checkpoints_path: Path | str) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Return recorded ``(person, checkpoint)`` pairs from ``checkpoints.csv``."""
    path = Path(checkpoints_path)
    if not path.exists() or path.stat().st_size == 0:
        return set()
    with path.open("r", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        if not reader.fieldnames:
            return set()
        return {
            (row["person"].strip().lower(), row["checkpoint"].strip().upper())
            for row in reader
            if row.get("person") and row.get("checkpoint")
        }


def append_checkpoint_row(checkpoints_path: Path | str, row: dict[str, str]) -> Path:
    """Create or append a validated row to ``checkpoints.csv``."""
    path = Path(checkpoints_path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    needs_header = not path.exists() or path.stat().st_size == 0

    if not needs_header:
        with path.open("r", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as handle:
            reader = csv.reader(handle)
            header = next(reader, None)
        if header is None:
            needs_header = True
        elif tuple(header) != CHECKPOINT_FIELDS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{path} has unexpected columns {header!r}; expected {list(CHECKPOINT_FIELDS)!r}"
            )

    normalized_row = {field: row.get(field, "") for field in CHECKPOINT_FIELDS}
    mode = "w" if needs_header else "a"
    with path.open(mode, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=CHECKPOINT_FIELDS, lineterminator="\n")
        if needs_header:
            writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerow(normalized_row)
    return path


def _ring_bell(out: TextIO, enabled: bool) -> None:
    if enabled:
        out.write("\a")
        out.flush()


def _format_mm_ss(seconds: float) -> str:
    total = max(0, int(round(seconds)))
    minutes, secs = divmod(total, 60)
    return f"{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}"


def run_countdown(
    steps: Sequence[ProtocolStep],
    *,
    out: TextIO = sys.stdout,
    bell: bool = True,
    clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
) -> None:
    """Run an interactive second-by-second countdown through all protocol steps."""
    is_tty = hasattr(out, "isatty") and out.isatty()
    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, start=1):
        _ring_bell(out, bell)
        out.write(
            f"\n[{idx}/{len(steps)}] {step.label.upper()} ({int(round(step.duration_s))} s): "
            f"{step.instruction}\n"
        )
        out.flush()

        step_start = clock()
        step_end = step_start + step.duration_s
        last_shown: int | None = None
        while True:
            remaining = step_end - clock()
            if remaining <= 0:
                break
            rem_sec = max(1, math.ceil(remaining - 0.01))
            if rem_sec != last_shown:
                last_shown = rem_sec
                if is_tty:
                    out.write(f"\r  Pozostało: {_format_mm_ss(rem_sec)} ")
                    out.flush()
                elif rem_sec == int(round(step.duration_s)) or rem_sec in (30, 15, 5):
                    out.write(f"  Pozostało: {_format_mm_ss(rem_sec)}\n")
                    out.flush()
            sleep(min(0.25, remaining))

        if is_tty:
            out.write("\r  Pozostało: 00:00 — koniec kroku\n")
        else:
            out.write("  Koniec kroku.\n")
        out.flush()

    _ring_bell(out, bell)
    out.write("\nKoniec protokołu EEG. Możesz zatrzymać nagrywanie w BrainAccess Board.\n")
    out.flush()


def _prompt_until_valid(
    prompt: str,
    parser: Callable[[str], object],
    *,
    input_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    out: TextIO,
    default: str | None = None,
) -> object:
    while True:
        raw = input_fn(prompt)
        if not raw.strip() and default is not None:
            raw = default
        try:
            return parser(raw)
        except ValueError as exc:
            out.write(f"  Błąd: {exc}\n")
            out.flush()


def run_session(
    args: argparse.Namespace,
    *,
    input_fn: Callable[[str], str] = input,
    out: TextIO = sys.stdout,
    clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
    now_fn: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Path | dict[str, str] | None]:
    """Execute one checkpoint session and persist annotations + checkpoints row."""
    now_provider = now_fn or (lambda: datetime.now().astimezone())
    recordings_dir = Path(args.recordings_dir)
    checkpoints_path = (
        Path(args.checkpoints_file)
        if args.checkpoints_file
        else recordings_dir / "checkpoints.csv"
    )
    steps = build_steps(args.step_seconds, getattr(args, "monotony_min", 0.0) or 0.0)

    person = (
        normalize_person(args.person)
        if args.person
        else _prompt_until_valid(
            "Imię uczestnika (np. adi, filip, rudy, wojtek): ",
            normalize_person,
            input_fn=input_fn,
            out=out,
        )
    )
    checkpoint = (
        normalize_checkpoint(args.checkpoint)
        if args.checkpoint
        else _prompt_until_valid(
            "Punkt pomiarowy (T0 / T1 / T2): ",
            normalize_checkpoint,
            input_fn=input_fn,
            out=out,
        )
    )
    if checkpoint not in VALID_CHECKPOINTS:
        out.write(
            f"Uwaga: nietypowy punkt {checkpoint!r} (standardowe: {', '.join(VALID_CHECKPOINTS)}).\n"
        )

    prior = existing_checkpoints(checkpoints_path)
    if (person, checkpoint) in prior:
        out.write(
            f"Uwaga: w {checkpoints_path} istnieje już wpis dla ({person}, {checkpoint}).\n"
        )

    task_hint = f"{person}-{checkpoint.lower()}"
    include_watch = (person == "wojtek" and not args.no_watch) or args.watch

    kss: int | None = parse_kss(args.kss) if args.kss is not None else None
    body_battery = parse_optional_number(
        args.body_battery, "body_battery", min_value=0, max_value=100, integer=True
    )
    stress = parse_optional_number(
        args.stress, "stress", min_value=0, max_value=100, integer=True
    )
    heart_rate = parse_optional_number(
        args.heart_rate, "heart_rate", min_value=20, max_value=250, integer=True
    )
    hrv_ms = parse_optional_number(args.hrv_ms, "hrv_ms", min_value=1, max_value=500)

    out.write(
        "\n=== Punkt pomiarowy Wachta ===\n"
        f"Uczestnik: {person} | Punkt: {checkpoint}\n"
        f"Nazwa zadania w BrainAccess Board: {task_hint}\n"
        "Pamiętaj: w BrainAccess Board zapisz tylko plik .fif (bez kopii CSV).\n"
    )
    if include_watch:
        out.write(
            "Garmin Venu 2: uruchom Health Snapshot podczas 2 min spoczynku "
            "(eyes_open + eyes_closed).\n"
        )
    out.flush()

    started_epoch: float | None = None
    if args.no_countdown:
        started_at = args.started_at or now_provider().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    else:
        input_fn(
            f"\nWłącz nagrywanie w BrainAccess Board (zadanie '{task_hint}') "
            "i naciśnij Enter dokładnie w momencie startu..."
        )
        started_dt = now_provider()
        started_at = args.started_at or started_dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        started_epoch = started_dt.timestamp()
        run_countdown(
            steps,
            out=out,
            bell=not args.no_bell,
            clock=clock,
            sleep=sleep,
        )

    if kss is None:
        out.write(f"\n{KSS_GUIDE}\n")
        out.flush()
        kss = _prompt_until_valid(
            "Ocena senności KSS (1–9): ",
            parse_kss,
            input_fn=input_fn,
            out=out,
        )

    if include_watch:
        if args.body_battery is None:
            body_battery = _prompt_until_valid(
                "Garmin Body Battery (0–100, Enter aby pominąć): ",
                lambda v: parse_optional_number(
                    v, "body_battery", min_value=0, max_value=100, integer=True
                ),
                input_fn=input_fn,
                out=out,
            )
        if args.stress is None:
            stress = _prompt_until_valid(
                "Garmin Stress (0–100, Enter aby pominąć): ",
                lambda v: parse_optional_number(
                    v, "stress", min_value=0, max_value=100, integer=True
                ),
                input_fn=input_fn,
                out=out,
            )
        if args.heart_rate is None:
            heart_rate = _prompt_until_valid(
                "Garmin Tętno [bpm] (Enter aby pominąć): ",
                lambda v: parse_optional_number(
                    v, "heart_rate", min_value=20, max_value=250, integer=True
                ),
                input_fn=input_fn,
                out=out,
            )
        if args.hrv_ms is None:
            hrv_ms = _prompt_until_valid(
                "Garmin HRV [ms] (Enter aby pominąć): ",
                lambda v: parse_optional_number(v, "hrv_ms", min_value=1, max_value=500),
                input_fn=input_fn,
                out=out,
            )

    recording_path: Path | None = Path(args.recording) if args.recording else None
    if recording_path is None and not args.no_prompt_recording:
        candidates = find_candidate_recordings(
            recordings_dir, person, checkpoint, started_epoch=started_epoch
        )
        # Only suggest a file that belongs to this session (task name or written after the start);
        # otherwise Enter would attach the annotations to an older recording of the same person.
        token = f"{person.lower()}-{checkpoint.lower()}"
        strong = [
            c for c in candidates
            if token in c.name.lower()
            or (started_epoch is not None and c.stat().st_mtime >= started_epoch - 5.0)
        ]
        if not strong:
            out.write(
                f"\nNie widzę nagrania z zadaniem '{token}' w {recordings_dir}. "
                "Skopiuj plik z BrainAccess Board do tego folderu i wpisz jego ścieżkę "
                "(Enter = bez nagrania).\n"
            )
        default_rec = str(strong[0]) if strong else ""
        hint = f" [domyślnie: {strong[0].name}]" if strong else ""
        entered = input_fn(
            f"Ścieżka do pliku .fif{hint} (Enter = domyślny / brak): "
        ).strip()
        if entered:
            recording_path = Path(entered)
        elif default_rec:
            recording_path = Path(default_rec)

    annotations_written: Path | None = None
    if args.annotations_out:
        annotations_written = write_annotations(args.annotations_out, steps)
    elif recording_path is not None:
        target = annotations_path_for(recording_path)
        overwrite = True
        if target.exists():
            answer = input_fn(
                f"Plik adnotacji {target.name} już istnieje. Nadpisać? [t/N]: "
            ).strip().lower()
            overwrite = answer in ("t", "tak", "y", "yes")
            if not overwrite:
                out.write("Adnotacje nie zostały zapisane (istniejący plik bez zmian).\n")
        if overwrite:
            annotations_written = write_annotations(target, steps)

    notes = args.notes
    if notes is None and not args.no_prompt_notes:
        notes = input_fn("Uwagi (Enter aby pominąć): ").strip()

    row = {
        "person": person,
        "checkpoint": checkpoint,
        "started_at": started_at,
        "kss": str(kss),
        "body_battery": str(body_battery),
        "stress": str(stress),
        "heart_rate": str(heart_rate),
        "hrv_ms": str(hrv_ms),
        "recording_file": (
            format_recording_ref(recording_path, recordings_dir)
            if recording_path is not None
            else ""
        ),
        "notes": (notes or "").strip(),
    }
    saved_checkpoints = append_checkpoint_row(checkpoints_path, row)

    out.write("\nZapisano:\n")
    out.write(f"  - {saved_checkpoints}\n")
    if annotations_written is not None:
        out.write(f"  - {annotations_written}\n")
    else:
        out.write(
            "  - (adnotacje nie zostały zapisane — brak ścieżki do .fif; "
            "użyj --recording lub --annotations-out)\n"
        )
    out.flush()

    return {
        "checkpoints_path": saved_checkpoints,
        "annotations_path": annotations_written,
        "row": row,
    }


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="python -m wachta.session",
        description="Narzędzie punktu pomiarowego Wachty (protokół 3x60 s, KSS, adnotacje i checkpoints.csv).",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--person", help="Imię uczestnika (np. adi, filip, rudy, wojtek)")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint", help="Punkt pomiarowy (np. T0, T1, T2)")
    parser.add_argument("--kss", type=int, help="Ocena senności KSS 1–9")
    parser.add_argument("--body-battery", help="Garmin Body Battery (0–100)")
    parser.add_argument("--stress", help="Garmin Stress (0–100)")
    parser.add_argument("--heart-rate", help="Tętno [bpm]")
    parser.add_argument("--hrv-ms", help="HRV [ms]")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--watch",
        action="store_true",
        help="Pytaj o odczyty z zegarka Garmin nawet poza profilem Wojtka",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-watch",
        action="store_true",
        help="Pomiń interaktywne pytania o odczyty z zegarka Garmin",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--recording", help="Ścieżka do pliku nagrania .fif")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--annotations-out",
        help="Jawna ścieżka wyjściowa dla pliku adnotacji CSV",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--recordings-dir",
        default=str(DEFAULT_RECORDINGS_DIR),
        help="Folder z nagraniami (domyślnie: data/recordings)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--checkpoints-file",
        help="Ścieżka do checkpoints.csv (domyślnie: <recordings-dir>/checkpoints.csv)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--step-seconds",
        type=float,
        default=DEFAULT_STEP_SECONDS,
        help="Czas trwania każdego z 3 bloków EEG w sekundach (domyślnie: 60)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--monotony-min",
        type=float,
        default=0.0,
        help="Dodaj na końcu blok monotonii o tej długości w minutach (nocny protokół: 10)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--started-at",
        help="Znacznik czasu rozpoczęcia ISO-8601 (przydatne z --no-countdown)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--notes",
        default=None,
        help="Dodatkowe uwagi do wiersza w checkpoints.csv",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-countdown",
        action="store_true",
        help="Pomiń odliczanie w czasie rzeczywistym (np. przy uzupełnianiu po nagraniu)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-bell",
        action="store_true",
        help="Wyłącz sygnał dźwiękowy terminala przy zmianie kroku",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-prompt-recording",
        action="store_true",
        help="Nie pytaj interaktywnie o plik .fif, gdy --recording nie jest podane",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-prompt-notes",
        action="store_true",
        help="Nie pytaj interaktywnie o uwagi, gdy --notes nie jest podane",
    )
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = build_parser()
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        run_session(args)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        sys.stdout.write("\nPrzerwano sesję pomiarową (nic nie zapisano).\n")
        return 130
    except ValueError as exc:
        sys.stderr.write(f"Błąd: {exc}\n")
        return 2
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## FILE: `src/wachta/sources.py`
```
"""Where EEG samples come from: a recording played back, or BrainAccess Board live.

Every source exposes ``chunks()``, an async iterator of arrays shaped (8, n) in
volts with channels ordered as ``config.CHANNELS``, plus ``name`` and ``live``.
"""

import asyncio
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import AsyncIterator

import numpy as np

from .config import CHANNELS
from .recordings import find_recordings, load_eeg

# Unit names used by BrainAccess Board metadata, mapped to volts.
_UNIT_TO_VOLTS = {"microvolts": 1e-6, "uV": 1e-6, "millivolts": 1e-3, "mV": 1e-3, "volts": 1.0, "V": 1.0}


class ReplaySource:
    """Plays an array of samples at ``speed`` times real time, optionally in a loop.

    With ``end_s`` the replay is the segment ``start_s``..``end_s`` and loops inside it, so a team member can be
    kept on a calm stretch of a recording. Without it the whole recording loops from the beginning.
    """

    live = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        eeg_volts: np.ndarray,
        sfreq: float,
        name: str = "nagranie",
        speed: float = 1.0,
        start_s: float = 0.0,
        loop: bool = True,
        chunk_s: float = 0.25,
        end_s: float | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if speed <= 0:
            raise ValueError("speed must be positive")
        if end_s is not None and end_s <= start_s:
            raise ValueError("end_s must be later than start_s")
        self.eeg = eeg_volts
        self.sfreq = sfreq
        self.name = name
        self.speed = speed
        self.start_s = start_s
        self.loop = loop
        self.end_s = end_s
        self.chunk = max(1, int(round(chunk_s * sfreq)))

    @classmethod
    def from_file(cls, path: Path | str, **kwargs) -> "ReplaySource":
        eeg, sfreq = load_eeg(path)
        return cls(eeg, sfreq, **kwargs)

    async def chunks(self) -> AsyncIterator[np.ndarray]:
        first = min(int(self.start_s * self.sfreq), max(self.eeg.shape[1] - self.chunk, 0))
        stop = self.eeg.shape[1] if self.end_s is None else min(self.eeg.shape[1], int(self.end_s * self.sfreq))
        restart = 0 if self.end_s is None else first
        position = first
        interval = self.chunk / self.sfreq / self.speed
        due = time.monotonic()
        while True:
            if position >= stop:
                if not self.loop:
                    return
                position = restart
            yield self.eeg[:, position : min(position + self.chunk, stop)]
            position += self.chunk
            due += interval
            await asyncio.sleep(max(0.0, due - time.monotonic()))


class BoardSource:
    """Reads new samples from the database of a running BrainAccess Board session.

    Needs the ``live`` extra (``pip install -e ".[live]"``), the Board app running
    and the headset streaming. Not exercised by the test suite: it needs hardware.
    """

    live = True

    def __init__(self, name: str = "na żywo", poll_s: float = 0.25, sfreq: float = 250.0, stale_s: float = 3.0) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.poll_s = poll_s
        self.sfreq = sfreq
        self.stale_s = stale_s       # no new samples for this long: report an error and reconnect

    @staticmethod
    def _connect():
        try:
            import brainaccess_board as bb
        except ImportError as error:
            raise RuntimeError('Brak biblioteki brainaccess-board. Zainstaluj: pip install -e ".[live]"') from error
        database, ok = bb.db_connect()
        if not ok or database is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Nie widzę aktywnej sesji BrainAccess Board. Uruchom Board i połącz czepek.")
        return database

    @staticmethod
    def _pick_device(database) -> tuple[str, list[int], np.ndarray, float]:
        """Find the device that has all eight channels; return its id, row order and unit scale."""
        # ReadDB closes its handle right after opening, so reopen it for the device listing.
        database._connect()
        try:
            devices = database._list_devices()["data"]
        finally:
            database._close()
        seen = {}
        for device, info in devices.items():
            names = [str(ch).strip() for ch in info["channels"]]
            seen[device] = names
            lookup = {ch.upper(): i for i, ch in enumerate(names)}
            if all(ch.upper() in lookup for ch in CHANNELS):
                rows = [lookup[ch.upper()] for ch in CHANNELS]
                units = [str(info["channels_unit"][i]) for i in rows]
                scale = np.array([_UNIT_TO_VOLTS.get(u, 1.0) for u in units]).reshape(-1, 1)
                return device, rows, scale, float(info["srate"])
        raise RuntimeError(f"Żadne urządzenie w Board nie ma kanałów {CHANNELS}. Znalezione: {seen}")

    async def chunks(self) -> AsyncIterator[np.ndarray]:
        database = await asyncio.to_thread(self._connect)
        device, rows, scale, srate = await asyncio.to_thread(self._pick_device, database)
        if abs(srate - self.sfreq) > 0.5:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Board podaje {srate} Hz, a pipeline oczekuje {self.sfreq} Hz.")

        # Skip whatever was recorded before we connected.
        backlog = await asyncio.to_thread(database.get_data_since, device, 0.0)
        last = backlog["last_timestamp"] if backlog else 0.0
        last_data = time.monotonic()
        while True:
            fresh = await asyncio.to_thread(database.get_data_since, device, last)
            if fresh:
                last = fresh["last_timestamp"]
                last_data = time.monotonic()
                yield np.asarray(fresh["data"], dtype=float)[rows] * scale
            elif time.monotonic() - last_data > self.stale_s:
                # A stopped stream must not look like a calm worker: surface it and let the worker reconnect.
                raise RuntimeError(f"Brak nowych próbek z Board od {self.stale_s:.0f} s. Sprawdź czepek i Board.")
            await asyncio.sleep(self.poll_s)


def resolve_recording(spec: str) -> Path:
    """Accept a path to a ``.fif`` file or the task name of a team recording (e.g. ``filip``)."""
    path = Path(spec)
    if path.exists():
        return path
    recordings = find_recordings()
    if spec in recordings:
        return recordings[spec]
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Nie znam nagrania '{spec}'. Dostępne: {', '.join(recordings) or 'brak'}")
```

---
## FILE: `src/wachta/summary.py`
```
"""Merge and summarize team checkpoints (KSS + Garmin Venu 2) and PVT-B results.

Combines ``data/recordings/checkpoints.csv`` (written by ``python -m wachta.session``)
and ``data/recordings/pvt.csv`` (written by ``python -m wachta.pvt``) by
``(person, checkpoint)``, computes within-person changes relative to ``T0``, prints a
slide-ready Markdown table in Polish and optionally writes a merged CSV and PNG chart.

Run with:
    python -m wachta.summary
    python experiments/summarize-checkpoints.py --figure docs/img/checkpoints-summary.png
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import Any, Sequence, TextIO

from .pvt import DEFAULT_RESULTS_FILE as DEFAULT_PVT_FILE
from .session import DEFAULT_RECORDINGS_DIR, VALID_CHECKPOINTS, normalize_checkpoint, normalize_person

DEFAULT_CHECKPOINTS_FILE = DEFAULT_RECORDINGS_DIR / "checkpoints.csv"

MERGED_FIELDS = (
    "person",
    "checkpoint",
    "started_at",
    "kss",
    "delta_kss",
    "median_rt_ms",
    "delta_median_rt_ms",
    "mean_rt_ms",
    "lapses",
    "false_starts",
    "mean_speed",
    "performance_score",
    "body_battery",
    "delta_body_battery",
    "stress",
    "heart_rate",
    "hrv_ms",
    "delta_hrv_ms",
    "recording_file",
    "notes",
)


def _opt_int(value: Any) -> int | None:
    if value in (None, ""):
        return None
    return int(round(float(str(value).strip().replace(",", "."))))


def _opt_float(value: Any, digits: int = 3) -> float | None:
    if value in (None, ""):
        return None
    return round(float(str(value).strip().replace(",", ".")), digits)


def _checkpoint_sort_key(cp: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    if cp in VALID_CHECKPOINTS:
        return (VALID_CHECKPOINTS.index(cp), cp)
    if cp.startswith("T") and cp[1:].isdigit():
        return (int(cp[1:]), cp)
    return (999, cp)


def _read_keyed_csv(path: Path | str) -> dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, str]]:
    file_path = Path(path)
    if not file_path.exists() or file_path.stat().st_size == 0:
        return {}
    records: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, str]] = {}
    with file_path.open("r", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        for row in reader:
            raw_person = (row.get("person") or "").strip()
            raw_cp = (row.get("checkpoint") or "").strip()
            if not raw_person or not raw_cp:
                continue
            key = (normalize_person(raw_person), normalize_checkpoint(raw_cp))
            records[key] = row
    return records


def _diff_int(current: int | None, base: int | None) -> int | None:
    if current is None or base is None:
        return None
    return current - base


def _diff_float(current: float | None, base: float | None, digits: int = 1) -> float | None:
    if current is None or base is None:
        return None
    return round(current - base, digits)


def load_and_merge(
    checkpoints_path: Path | str = DEFAULT_CHECKPOINTS_FILE,
    pvt_path: Path | str = DEFAULT_PVT_FILE,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Load ``checkpoints.csv`` and ``pvt.csv``, outer-join on ``(person, checkpoint)`` and compute deltas vs ``T0``."""
    cp_rows = _read_keyed_csv(checkpoints_path)
    pvt_rows = _read_keyed_csv(pvt_path)

    all_keys = sorted(
        set(cp_rows) | set(pvt_rows),
        key=lambda item: (item[0], _checkpoint_sort_key(item[1])),
    )

    merged: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for person, checkpoint in all_keys:
        cp = cp_rows.get((person, checkpoint), {})
        pv = pvt_rows.get((person, checkpoint), {})
        merged.append(
            {
                "person": person,
                "checkpoint": checkpoint,
                "started_at": (cp.get("started_at") or pv.get("started_at") or "").strip(),
                "kss": _opt_int(cp.get("kss")),
                "delta_kss": None,
                "median_rt_ms": _opt_float(pv.get("median_rt_ms"), 1),
                "delta_median_rt_ms": None,
                "mean_rt_ms": _opt_float(pv.get("mean_rt_ms"), 1),
                "lapses": _opt_int(pv.get("lapses")),
                "false_starts": _opt_int(pv.get("false_starts")),
                "mean_speed": _opt_float(pv.get("mean_speed"), 3),
                "performance_score": _opt_float(pv.get("performance_score"), 3),
                "body_battery": _opt_int(cp.get("body_battery")),
                "delta_body_battery": None,
                "stress": _opt_int(cp.get("stress")),
                "heart_rate": _opt_int(cp.get("heart_rate")),
                "hrv_ms": _opt_float(cp.get("hrv_ms"), 1),
                "delta_hrv_ms": None,
                "recording_file": (cp.get("recording_file") or "").strip(),
                "notes": (cp.get("notes") or "").strip(),
            }
        )

    t0_by_person = {row["person"]: row for row in merged if row["checkpoint"] == "T0"}
    for row in merged:
        base = t0_by_person.get(row["person"])
        if base is None:
            continue
        row["delta_kss"] = _diff_int(row["kss"], base["kss"])
        row["delta_median_rt_ms"] = _diff_float(row["median_rt_ms"], base["median_rt_ms"], 1)
        row["delta_body_battery"] = _diff_int(row["body_battery"], base["body_battery"])
        row["delta_hrv_ms"] = _diff_float(row["hrv_ms"], base["hrv_ms"], 1)

    return merged


def _pl_num(value: int | float | None, digits: int | None = None, signed: bool = False) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return "—"
    if digits is None or isinstance(value, int):
        fmt = f"{int(value):+d}" if signed else f"{int(value):d}"
        return fmt
    fmt = f"{float(value):+.{digits}f}" if signed else f"{float(value):.{digits}f}"
    return fmt.replace(".", ",")


def _with_delta(
    value: int | float | None,
    delta: int | float | None,
    *,
    checkpoint: str,
    digits: int | None = None,
) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return "—"
    base_str = _pl_num(value, digits=digits, signed=False)
    if checkpoint == "T0" or delta is None:
        return base_str
    return f"{base_str} ({_pl_num(delta, digits=digits, signed=True)})"


def format_markdown_table(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """Format merged checkpoint rows as a Markdown table in Polish."""
    lines = [
        "| Osoba | Punkt | KSS (1–9) | PVT mediana [ms] | PVT lapsy | PVT 1/RT [1/s] | "
        "Body Battery | Stress | Tętno [bpm] | HRV [ms] |",
        "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|",
    ]
    for row in rows:
        cp = row["checkpoint"]
        lines.append(
            f"| {row['person']} | {cp} | "
            f"{_with_delta(row['kss'], row['delta_kss'], checkpoint=cp)} | "
            f"{_with_delta(row['median_rt_ms'], row['delta_median_rt_ms'], checkpoint=cp, digits=1)} | "
            f"{_pl_num(row['lapses'])} | "
            f"{_pl_num(row['mean_speed'], digits=2)} | "
            f"{_with_delta(row['body_battery'], row['delta_body_battery'], checkpoint=cp)} | "
            f"{_pl_num(row['stress'])} | "
            f"{_pl_num(row['heart_rate'])} | "
            f"{_with_delta(row['hrv_ms'], row['delta_hrv_ms'], checkpoint=cp, digits=1)} |"
        )
    return "\n".join(lines)


def write_merged_csv(path: Path | str, rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> Path:
    """Write merged rows to a CSV file with ``MERGED_FIELDS`` columns."""
    out_path = Path(path)
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=MERGED_FIELDS, lineterminator="\n")
        writer.writeheader()
        for row in rows:
            writer.writerow(
                {field: "" if row.get(field) is None else row.get(field) for field in MERGED_FIELDS}
            )
    return out_path


def _load_pil_font(size: int, bold: bool = False):
    from PIL import ImageFont

    candidates = (
        (
            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf",
            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSans-Bold.ttf",
        )
        if bold
        else (
            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf",
            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSans-Regular.ttf",
        )
    )
    for path in candidates:
        if Path(path).exists():
            try:
                return ImageFont.truetype(path, size=size), True
            except OSError:
                continue
    return ImageFont.load_default(), False


_PL_ASCII = str.maketrans("ąćęłńóśźżĄĆĘŁŃÓŚŹŻ–—", "acelnoszzACELNOSZZ--")


def _save_figure_pillow(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], path: Path) -> None:
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

    width, height = 1080, 420
    img = Image.new("RGB", (width, height), (252, 252, 251))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    font_title, has_utf8 = _load_pil_font(15, bold=True)
    font_panel, _ = _load_pil_font(12, bold=True)
    font_body, _ = _load_pil_font(11, bold=False)

    def txt(s: str) -> str:
        return s if has_utf8 else s.translate(_PL_ASCII)

    ink = (15, 15, 15)
    muted = (115, 113, 108)
    grid = (225, 224, 217)
    palette = [
        (42, 120, 214),
        (208, 59, 59),
        (39, 150, 92),
        (214, 134, 32),
    ]

    draw.text(
        (28, 16),
        txt("Punkty pomiarowe zespołu (T0 / T1 / T2): KSS, PVT-B i Garmin Venu 2"),
        fill=ink,
        font=font_title,
    )
    draw.text(
        (28, 38),
        txt("Każda linia to jedna osoba; porównanie zmian w trakcie pracy"),
        fill=muted,
        font=font_body,
    )

    panels = [
        ("Senność KSS (1–9)", "kss", 1.0, 9.0),
        ("PVT-B mediana RT [ms]", "median_rt_ms", 180.0, 500.0),
        ("Garmin Body Battery (0–100)", "body_battery", 0.0, 100.0),
    ]
    checkpoints = list(VALID_CHECKPOINTS)
    people = sorted({r["person"] for r in rows})
    by_key = {(r["person"], r["checkpoint"]): r for r in rows}

    panel_w = 290
    top, bottom = 90, 355
    for idx, (title, field, vmin, vmax) in enumerate(panels):
        left = 54 + idx * (panel_w + 54)
        right = left + panel_w
        draw.text((left, top - 24), txt(title), fill=ink, font=font_panel)
        for frac in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
            y = int(bottom - frac * (bottom - top))
            draw.line([(left, y), (right, y)], fill=grid, width=1)
            val = vmin + frac * (vmax - vmin)
            draw.text((left - 36, y - 7), f"{val:.0f}", fill=muted, font=font_body)

        xs = [int(left + i * (panel_w // max(1, len(checkpoints) - 1))) for i in range(len(checkpoints))]
        for x, cp in zip(xs, checkpoints):
            draw.text((x - 8, bottom + 8), cp, fill=muted, font=font_body)

        for p_idx, person in enumerate(people):
            color = palette[p_idx % len(palette)]
            pts: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
            for x, cp in zip(xs, checkpoints):
                rec = by_key.get((person, cp))
                val = rec.get(field) if rec else None
                if val is None:
                    continue
                norm = max(0.0, min(1.0, (float(val) - vmin) / max(1e-6, vmax - vmin)))
                y = int(bottom - norm * (bottom - top))
                pts.append((x, y))
            if len(pts) >= 2:
                draw.line(pts, fill=color, width=3)
            for x, y in pts:
                draw.ellipse((x - 4, y - 4, x + 4, y + 4), fill=color)

    for p_idx, person in enumerate(people):
        color = palette[p_idx % len(palette)]
        lx = 54 + p_idx * 140
        draw.rectangle((lx, height - 26, lx + 16, height - 16), fill=color)
        draw.text((lx + 22, height - 28), txt(person), fill=ink, font=font_body)

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    img.save(path, format="PNG", optimize=True)


def save_summary_figure(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], path: Path | str) -> Path:
    """Save a compact 3-panel PNG chart (< 300 kB) of KSS, PVT median RT and Garmin Body Battery."""
    out_path = Path(path)
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        import matplotlib

        matplotlib.use("Agg")
        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    except ImportError:
        _save_figure_pillow(rows, out_path)
        return out_path

    checkpoints = list(VALID_CHECKPOINTS)
    people = sorted({r["person"] for r in rows})
    by_key = {(r["person"], r["checkpoint"]): r for r in rows}
    palette = ["#2a78d6", "#d03b3b", "#27965c", "#d68620"]

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(10.2, 3.6), facecolor="#fcfcfb")
    specs = [
        ("Senność KSS (1–9)", "kss", (1, 9)),
        ("PVT-B mediana RT [ms]", "median_rt_ms", None),
        ("Garmin Body Battery (0–100)", "body_battery", (0, 100)),
    ]
    for ax, (title, field, ylim) in zip(axes, specs):
        ax.set_facecolor("#fcfcfb")
        for p_idx, person in enumerate(people):
            xs, ys = [], []
            for idx, cp in enumerate(checkpoints):
                rec = by_key.get((person, cp))
                val = rec.get(field) if rec else None
                if val is not None:
                    xs.append(idx)
                    ys.append(float(val))
            if xs:
                ax.plot(xs, ys, marker="o", lw=1.8, color=palette[p_idx % len(palette)], label=person)
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(checkpoints)), checkpoints)
        ax.set_title(title, loc="left", fontsize=9.5, fontweight="bold")
        if ylim:
            ax.set_ylim(*ylim)
        ax.grid(axis="y", color="#e1e0d9", lw=0.8)
        for side in ("top", "right"):
            ax.spines[side].set_visible(False)

    if people:
        axes[0].legend(frameon=False, fontsize=8.5)
    fig.suptitle(
        "Punkty pomiarowe zespołu (T0 / T1 / T2): KSS, PVT-B i Garmin Venu 2",
        x=0.01,
        y=0.98,
        ha="left",
        fontsize=11.5,
        fontweight="bold",
    )
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.91))
    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=120, facecolor="#fcfcfb")
    plt.close(fig)
    return out_path


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="python -m wachta.summary",
        description="Podsumowanie punktów pomiarowych (checkpoints.csv + pvt.csv): tabela Markdown, CSV i wykres.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--checkpoints",
        default=str(DEFAULT_CHECKPOINTS_FILE),
        help="Ścieżka do checkpoints.csv (domyślnie: data/recordings/checkpoints.csv)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--pvt",
        default=str(DEFAULT_PVT_FILE),
        help="Ścieżka do pvt.csv (domyślnie: data/recordings/pvt.csv)",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--out-csv", help="Zapisz połączoną tabelę do pliku CSV")
    parser.add_argument("--figure", help="Zapisz wykres PNG (np. docs/img/checkpoints-summary.png)")
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None, *, out: TextIO = sys.stdout) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    rows = load_and_merge(args.checkpoints, args.pvt)
    if not rows:
        out.write(
            f"Brak danych w {args.checkpoints} i {args.pvt}. "
            "Uruchom najpierw `python -m wachta.session` lub `python -m wachta.pvt`.\n"
        )
        return 0

    out.write(format_markdown_table(rows) + "\n")
    if args.out_csv:
        write_merged_csv(args.out_csv, rows)
        out.write(f"\nZapisano połączony CSV: {args.out_csv}\n")
    if args.figure:
        save_summary_figure(rows, args.figure)
        out.write(f"Zapisano wykres: {args.figure}\n")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
